{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_vrtx_01H8nKY2pbooT3kTHTnZNgVi","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01T4f6mKM3rKqQVHXUbjGHLp","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/ac_opf_solver.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nAC Optimal Power Flow Solver for ISO Base Case Planning\nUses CasADi and IPOPT to find least-cost AC-feasible operating point\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nimport casadi as ca\nfrom typing import Dict, List, Tuple\nimport sys\n\ndef load_network(filepath: str) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Load MATPOWER format network data\"\"\"\n    with open(filepath, 'r') as f:\n        return json.load(f)\n\ndef build_admittance_matrix(network: Dict, baseMVA: float) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:\n    \"\"\"Build admittance matrix from branch data\n\n    Returns:\n        Y: complex admittance matrix (sparse representation)\n        Yf, Yt: admittance matrices for from/to buses\n    \"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    branches = np.array(network['branch'])\n    n_bus = len(network['bus'])\n\n    # MATPOWER branch format: [fbus tbus r x b rateA rateB rateC ratio angle status angmin angmax]\n    fbus = branches[:, 0].astype(int) - 1  # Convert to 0-indexing\n    tbus = branches[:, 1].astype(int) - 1\n    r = branches[:, 2]\n    x = branches[:, 3]\n    b = branches[:, 4]\n    ratio = branches[:, 8]  # transformer ratio\n    angle_shift = branches[:, 9] * np.pi / 180  # phase shift in radians\n    status = branches[:, 10]\n\n    # Series impedance and admittance\n    z = r + 1j * x\n    y_series = 1.0 / z\n\n    # Transformer ratios (default 1.0 for lines)\n    T = np.where(ratio > 0, ratio, 1.0) * np.exp(1j * angle_shift)\n\n    # Build full admittance matrix\n    Y = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n\n    for idx in range(len(branches)):\n        if status[idx] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        i, j = fbus[idx], tbus[idx]\n        t = T[idx]\n        y = y_series[idx]\n\n        # Shunt admittance\n        y_shunt_i = 1j * b[idx] / 2\n        y_shunt_j = 1j * b[idx] / 2\n\n        # Off-diagonal elements\n        Y[i, j] -= y / np.conj(t)\n        Y[j, i] -= y / t\n\n        # Diagonal elements\n        Y[i, i] += y / (np.conj(t) * t) + y_shunt_i\n        Y[j, j] += y + y_shunt_j\n\n    # Add bus shunts (none in this dataset but included for completeness)\n    buses = np.array(network['bus'])\n    gs = buses[:, 4]\n    bs = buses[:, 5]\n    Y += np.diag(gs + 1j * bs)\n\n    return Y, fbus, tbus, T, y_series, status\n\ndef solve_ac_opf(network: Dict) -> Tuple[Dict, Dict]:\n    \"\"\"Solve AC OPF using CasADi and IPOPT\n\n    Returns:\n        solution: solver solution with variables\n        info: solver info dict\n    \"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    buses = np.array(network['bus'])\n    gens = np.array(network['gen'])\n    gencost = np.array(network['gencost'])\n    branches = np.array(network['branch'])\n\n    n_bus = len(buses)\n    n_gen = len(gens)\n    n_branch = len(branches)\n\n    # MATPOWER indices (convert to 0-based)\n    bus_i = buses[:, 0].astype(int) - 1\n    bus_type = buses[:, 1].astype(int)  # 1=PQ, 2=PV, 3=ref\n    pd_mw = buses[:, 2]  # Load P\n    qd_mvar = buses[:, 3]  # Load Q\n    vm_max = buses[:, 11]\n    vm_min = buses[:, 12]\n\n    gen_bus = gens[:, 0].astype(int) - 1\n    gen_pg = gens[:, 1]  # Initial P\n    gen_qg = gens[:, 2]  # Initial Q\n    gen_qmax = gens[:, 3]\n    gen_qmin = gens[:, 4]\n    gen_status = gens[:, 7]\n    gen_pmax = gens[:, 8]\n    gen_pmin = gens[:, 9]\n\n    # Cost function: c2*P^2 + c1*P + c0\n    cost_type = gencost[:, 0].astype(int)\n    cost_n = gencost[:, 3].astype(int)\n    c2 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    c1 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    c0 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if cost_type[i] == 2:  # Polynomial cost\n            n = cost_n[i]\n            if n >= 3:\n                c2[i] = gencost[i, 4]\n                c1[i] = gencost[i, 5] if n >= 3 else 0\n                c0[i] = gencost[i, 6] if n >= 3 else 0\n\n    # Build admittance matrix\n    Y, fbus, tbus, T, y_series, branch_status = build_admittance_matrix(network)\n\n    # Branch limits\n    branch_limit = branches[:, 5]  # rateA in MVA\n\n    # Reference bus (slack bus)\n    ref_bus = np.where(bus_type == 3)[0]\n    if len(ref_bus) == 0:\n        ref_bus = np.array([0])\n    else:\n        ref_bus = ref_bus[0]\n\n    # ===== Create Optimization Problem =====\n    opti = ca.Opti()\n\n    # Variables: voltage magnitudes and angles\n    vm = opti.variable(n_bus)  # voltage magnitudes (p.u.)\n    va = opti.variable(n_bus)  # voltage angles (radians)\n\n    # Generator dispatch\n    pg = opti.variable(n_gen)  # real power\n    qg = opti.variable(n_gen)  # reactive power\n\n    # ===== Objective: Minimize generation cost =====\n    cost = ca.sum1(c2 * pg**2 + c1 * pg + c0)\n    opti.minimize(cost)\n\n    # ===== Constraints =====\n\n    # Voltage magnitude bounds\n    opti.subject_to(vm >= vm_min)\n    opti.subject_to(vm <= vm_max)\n\n    # Generator bounds (only active generators)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[i] == 1:\n            opti.subject_to(pg[i] >= gen_pmin[i] / baseMVA)\n            opti.subject_to(pg[i] <= gen_pmax[i] / baseMVA)\n            opti.subject_to(qg[i] >= gen_qmin[i] / baseMVA)\n            opti.subject_to(qg[i] <= gen_qmax[i] / baseMVA)\n        else:\n            opti.subject_to(pg[i] == 0)\n            opti.subject_to(qg[i] == 0)\n\n    # Reference bus angle\n    opti.subject_to(va[ref_bus] == 0)\n\n    # Power flow constraints (nodal balance)\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        # Bus voltage as complex number\n        V_i = vm[i] * ca.exp(1j * va[i])\n\n        # Generation and load\n        p_gen = 0\n        q_gen = 0\n        for g in range(n_gen):\n            if gen_bus[g] == i:\n                p_gen += pg[g]\n                q_gen += qg[g]\n\n        # Convert load from MW/MVAr to p.u.\n        p_load = pd_mw[i] / baseMVA\n        q_load = qd_mvar[i] / baseMVA\n\n        # Power balance: S_gen - S_load = sum of branch flows\n        p_mismatch = 0\n        q_mismatch = 0\n\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            if Y[i, j] != 0:\n                V_j = vm[j] * ca.exp(1j * va[j])\n                S_ij = ca.conj(V_i) * (Y[i, j] * V_j)\n                p_mismatch += ca.real(S_ij)\n                q_mismatch += ca.imag(S_ij)\n\n        opti.subject_to(p_gen - p_load == p_mismatch)\n        opti.subject_to(q_gen - q_load == q_mismatch)\n\n    # Branch flow limits (apparent power)\n    for idx in range(n_branch):\n        if branch_status[idx] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        i, j = fbus[idx], tbus[idx]\n        t = T[idx]\n        y = y_series[idx]\n        limit = branch_limit[idx] / baseMVA\n\n        V_i = vm[i] * ca.exp(1j * va[i])\n        V_j = vm[j] * ca.exp(1j * va[j])\n\n        # Power flow from i to j\n        S_ij = (y / (ca.conj(t) * t) + 1j * np.imag(Y[i, i])) * ca.conj(V_i) * V_i - \\\n               (y / ca.conj(t)) * ca.conj(V_i) * V_j\n\n        # Power flow from j to i\n        S_ji = (y + 1j * np.imag(Y[j, j])) * ca.conj(V_j) * V_j - \\\n               (y / t) * ca.conj(V_j) * V_i\n\n        # Apparent power limits\n        opti.subject_to(ca.sqrt(ca.real(S_ij)**2 + ca.imag(S_ij)**2) <= limit)\n        opti.subject_to(ca.sqrt(ca.real(S_ji)**2 + ca.imag(S_ji)**2) <= limit)\n\n    # ===== Initial Guess =====\n    opti.set_initial(vm, 1.0)\n    opti.set_initial(va, 0.0)\n    opti.set_initial(pg, gen_pg / baseMVA)\n    opti.set_initial(qg, gen_qg / baseMVA)\n\n    # ===== Solver Setup =====\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt': {\n            'print_level': 0,\n            'max_iter': 3000,\n            'tol': 1e-6,\n            'acceptable_tol': 1e-4\n        }\n    }\n    opti.solver('ipopt', opts)\n\n    # Extract solution values\n    vm_val = np.array(sol.value(vm))\n    va_val = np.array(sol.value(va)) * 180 / np.pi  # convert to degrees\n    pg_val = np.array(sol.value(pg)) * baseMVA  # convert to MW\n    qg_val = np.array(sol.value(qg)) * baseMVA  # Calculate branch flows and find most loaded\n    branch_status = branches[:, 10]\n    fbus = branches[:, 0].astype(int) - 1\n    tbus = branches[:, 1].astype(int) - 1\n    limit_mva = branches[:, 5]  # Shunt admittance\n        y_shunt = 1j * b / 2\n\n        # Power flow from i to j\n        S_ij = (y / (np.conj(t) * t) + y_shunt) * np.conj(V_i) * V_i - \\\n               (y / np.conj(t)) * np.conj(V_i) * V_j\n\n        # Power flow from j to i\n        S_ji = (y + y_shunt) * np.conj(V_j) * V_j - \\\n               (y / t) * np.conj(V_j) * V_i\n\n        flow_from_mva = np.abs(S_ij) * baseMVA\n        flow_to_mva = np.abs(S_ji) * baseMVA\n\n        loading_pct = max(flow_from_mva, flow_to_mva) / limit_mva[idx] * 100\n\n        branch_data.append({\n            \"from_bus\": int(branches[idx, 0]),\n            \"to_bus\": int(branches[idx, 1]),\n            \"loading_pct\": float(loading_pct),\n            \"flow_from_MVA\": float(flow_from_mva),\n            \"flow_to_MVA\": float(flow_to_mva),\n            \"limit_MVA\": float(limit_mva[idx])\n        })\n\n    # Sort by loading and get top 10\n    branch_data.sort(key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n    most_loaded = branch_data[:10]\n\n    # Feasibility check\n    max_p_mismatch = 0\n    max_q_mismatch = 0\n    max_voltage_violation = 0\n    max_branch_overload = 0\n\n    # Check power balance\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        V_i = vm_val[i] * np.exp(1j * va_val[i] * np.pi / 180)\n        p_gen, q_gen = 0, 0\n        for g in range(n_gen):\n            if gen_bus[g] == i:\n                p_gen += pg_val[g]\n                q_gen += qg_val[g]\n\n        p_flow, q_flow = 0, 0\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            if Y[i, j] != 0:\n                V_j = vm_val[j] * np.exp(1j * va_val[j] * np.pi / 180)\n                S_ij = np.conj(V_i) * (Y[i, j] * V_j)\n                p_flow += np.real(S_ij) * baseMVA\n                q_flow += np.imag(S_ij) * baseMVA\n\n        p_mismatch = abs(p_gen - pd_mw[i] - p_flow)\n        q_mismatch = abs(q_gen - qd_mvar[i] - q_flow)\n        max_p_mismatch = max(max_p_mismatch, p_mismatch)\n        max_q_mismatch = max(max_q_mismatch, q_mismatch)\n\n    # Check voltage limits\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        violation = max(0, bus_vm_min[i] - vm_val[i]) + max(0, vm_val[i] - bus_vm_max[i])\n        max_voltage_violation = max(max_voltage_violation, violation)\n\n    # Check branch limits\n    for branch in branch_data:\n        overload = max(0, branch['flow_from_MVA'] - branch['limit_MVA'],\n                      branch['flow_to_MVA'] - branch['limit_MVA'])\n        max_branch_overload = max(max_branch_overload, overload)\n\n    # Build report\n    report = {\n        \"summary\": {\n            \"total_cost_per_hour\": float(cost_val),\n            \"total_load_MW\": float(total_load_mw),\n            \"total_load_MVAr\": float(total_load_mvar),\n            \"total_generation_MW\": float(total_gen_mw),\n            \"total_generation_MVAr\": float(total_gen_mvar),\n            \"total_losses_MW\": float(total_losses_mw),\n            \"solver_status\": status\n        },\n        \"generators\": generators,\n        \"buses\": buses_report,\n        \"most_loaded_branches\": most_loaded,\n        \"feasibility_check\": {\n            \"max_p_mismatch_MW\": float(max_p_mismatch),\n            \"max_q_mismatch_MVAr\": float(max_q_mismatch),\n            \"max_voltage_violation_pu\": float(max_voltage_violation),\n            \"max_branch_overload_MVA\": float(max_branch_overload)\n        }\n    }\n\n    return report\n\ndef main():\n    # MATPOWER branch format: [fbus tbus r x b rateA rateB rateC ratio angle status angmin angmax]\n    fbus = branches[:, 0].astype(int) - 1  # Convert to 0-indexing\n    tbus = branches[:, 1].astype(int) - 1\n    r = branches[:, 2]\n    x = branches[:, 3]\n    b = branches[:, 4]\n    ratio = branches[:, 8]  # transformer ratio\n    angle_shift = branches[:, 9] * np.pi / 180  # phase shift in radians\n    status = branches[:, 10]\n\n    # Series impedance and admittance\n    z = r + 1j * x\n    y_series = 1.0 / z\n\n    # Transformer ratios (default 1.0 for lines)\n    T = np.where(ratio > 0, ratio, 1.0) * np.exp(1j * angle_shift)\n\n    # Build full admittance matrix\n    Y = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n\n    for idx in range(len(branches)):\n        if status[idx] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        i, j = fbus[idx], tbus[idx]\n        t = T[idx]\n        y = y_series[idx]\n\n        # Shunt admittance\n        y_shunt_i = 1j * b[idx] / 2\n        y_shunt_j = 1j * b[idx] / 2\n\n        # Off-diagonal elements\n        Y[i, j] -= y / np.conj(t)\n        Y[j, i] -= y / t\n\n        # Diagonal elements\n        Y[i, i] += y / (np.conj(t) * t) + y_shunt_i\n        Y[j, j] += y + y_shunt_j\n\n    # Add bus shunts (none in this dataset but included for completeness)\n    buses = np.array(network['bus'])\n    gs = buses[:, 4]\n    bs = buses[:, 5]\n    Y += np.diag(gs + 1j * bs)\n\n    return Y, fbus, tbus, T, y_series, status\n\ndef solve_ac_opf(network: Dict) -> Tuple[Dict, Dict]:\n    \"\"\"Solve AC OPF using CasADi and IPOPT\n\n    Returns:\n        solution: solver solution with variables\n        info: solver info dict\n    \"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    buses = np.array(network['bus'])\n    gens = np.array(network['gen'])\n    gencost = np.array(network['gencost'])\n    branches = np.array(network['branch'])\n\n    n_bus = len(buses)\n    n_gen = len(gens)\n    n_branch = len(branches)\n\n    # MATPOWER indices (convert to 0-based)\n    bus_i = buses[:, 0].astype(int) - 1\n    bus_type = buses[:, 1].astype(int)  # 1=PQ, 2=PV, 3=ref\n    pd_mw = buses[:, 2]  # Load P\n    qd_mvar = buses[:, 3]  # Load Q\n    vm_max = buses[:, 11]\n    vm_min = buses[:, 12]\n\n    gen_bus = gens[:, 0].astype(int) - 1\n    gen_pg = gens[:, 1]  # Initial P\n    gen_qg = gens[:, 2]  # Initial Q\n    gen_qmax = gens[:, 3]\n    gen_qmin = gens[:, 4]\n    gen_status = gens[:, 7]\n    gen_pmax = gens[:, 8]\n    gen_pmin = gens[:, 9]\n\n    # Cost function: c2*P^2 + c1*P + c0\n    cost_type = gencost[:, 0].astype(int)\n    cost_n = gencost[:, 3].astype(int)\n    c2 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    c1 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    c0 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if cost_type[i] == 2:  # Polynomial cost\n            n = cost_n[i]\n            if n >= 3:\n                c2[i] = gencost[i, 4]\n                c1[i] = gencost[i, 5] if n >= 3 else 0\n                c0[i] = gencost[i, 6] if n >= 3 else 0\n\n    # Build admittance matrix\n    Y, fbus, tbus, T, y_series, branch_status = build_admittance_matrix(network)\n\n    # Branch limits\n    branch_limit = branches[:, 5]  # rateA in MVA\n\n    # Reference bus (slack bus)\n    ref_bus = np.where(bus_type == 3)[0]\n    if len(ref_bus) == 0:\n        ref_bus = np.array([0])\n    else:\n        ref_bus = ref_bus[0]\n\n    # ===== Create Optimization Problem =====\n    opti = ca.Opti()\n\n    # Variables: voltage magnitudes and angles\n    vm = opti.variable(n_bus)  # voltage magnitudes (p.u.)\n    va = opti.variable(n_bus)  # voltage angles (radians)\n\n    # Generator dispatch\n    pg = opti.variable(n_gen)  # real power\n    qg = opti.variable(n_gen)  # reactive power\n\n    # ===== Objective: Minimize generation cost =====\n    cost = ca.sum1(c2 * pg**2 + c1 * pg + c0)\n    opti.minimize(cost)\n\n    # ===== Constraints =====\n\n    # Voltage magnitude bounds\n    opti.subject_to(vm >= vm_min)\n    opti.subject_to(vm <= vm_max)\n\n    # Generator bounds (only active generators)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[i] == 1:\n            opti.subject_to(pg[i] >= gen_pmin[i] / baseMVA)\n            opti.subject_to(pg[i] <= gen_pmax[i] / baseMVA)\n            opti.subject_to(qg[i] >= gen_qmin[i] / baseMVA)\n            opti.subject_to(qg[i] <= gen_qmax[i] / baseMVA)\n        else:\n            opti.subject_to(pg[i] == 0)\n            opti.subject_to(qg[i] == 0)\n\n    # Reference bus angle\n    opti.subject_to(va[ref_bus] == 0)\n\n    # Power flow constraints (nodal balance)\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        # Bus voltage as complex number\n        V_i = vm[i] * ca.exp(1j * va[i])\n\n        # Generation and load\n        p_gen = 0\n        q_gen = 0\n        for g in range(n_gen):\n            if gen_bus[g] == i:\n                p_gen += pg[g]\n                q_gen += qg[g]\n\n        # Convert load from MW/MVAr to p.u.\n        p_load = pd_mw[i] / baseMVA\n        q_load = qd_mvar[i] / baseMVA\n\n        # Power balance: S_gen - S_load = sum of branch flows\n        p_mismatch = 0\n        q_mismatch = 0\n\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            if Y[i, j] != 0:\n                V_j = vm[j] * ca.exp(1j * va[j])\n                S_ij = ca.conj(V_i) * (Y[i, j] * V_j)\n                p_mismatch += ca.real(S_ij)\n                q_mismatch += ca.imag(S_ij)\n\n        opti.subject_to(p_gen - p_load == p_mismatch)\n        opti.subject_to(q_gen - q_load == q_mismatch)\n\n    # Branch flow limits (apparent power)\n    for idx in range(n_branch):\n        if branch_status[idx] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        i, j = fbus[idx], tbus[idx]\n        t = T[idx]\n        y = y_series[idx]\n        limit = branch_limit[idx] / baseMVA\n\n        V_i = vm[i] * ca.exp(1j * va[i])\n        V_j = vm[j] * ca.exp(1j * va[j])\n\n        # Power flow from i to j\n        S_ij = (y / (ca.conj(t) * t) + 1j * np.imag(Y[i, i])) * ca.conj(V_i) * V_i - \\\n               (y / ca.conj(t)) * ca.conj(V_i) * V_j\n\n        # Power flow from j to i\n        S_ji = (y + 1j * np.imag(Y[j, j])) * ca.conj(V_j) * V_j - \\\n               (y / t) * ca.conj(V_j) * V_i\n\n        # Apparent power limits\n        opti.subject_to(ca.sqrt(ca.real(S_ij)**2 + ca.imag(S_ij)**2) <= limit)\n        opti.subject_to(ca.sqrt(ca.real(S_ji)**2 + ca.imag(S_ji)**2) <= limit)\n\n    # ===== Initial Guess =====\n    opti.set_initial(vm, 1.0)\n    opti.set_initial(va, 0.0)\n    opti.set_initial(pg, gen_pg / baseMVA)\n    opti.set_initial(qg, gen_qg / baseMVA)\n\n    # ===== Solver Setup =====\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt': {\n            'print_level': 0,\n            'max_iter': 3000,\n            'tol': 1e-6,\n            'acceptable_tol': 1e-4\n        }\n    }\n    opti.solver('ipopt', opts)\n\n    # ===== Solve =====\n    try:\n        sol = opti.solve()\n        status = 'optimal' if sol.stats()['return_status'] == 'Solve_Succeeded' else 'suboptimal'\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Solver failed: {e}\", file=sys.stderr)\n        sol = opti.debug\n        status = 'failed'\n\n    return sol, opti, status, (vm, va, pg, qg), (buses, gens, branches, Y, baseMVA)\n\ndef generate_report(sol, opti, status, variables, network_info) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Generate report.json from solver solution\"\"\"\n    vm, va, pg, qg = variables\n    buses, gens, branches, Y, baseMVA = network_info\n\n    # Extract solution values\n    vm_val = np.array(sol.value(vm))\n    va_val = np.array(sol.value(va)) * 180 / np.pi  # convert to degrees\n    pg_val = np.array(sol.value(pg)) * baseMVA  # convert to MW\n    qg_val = np.array(sol.value(qg)) * baseMVA  # rateA\n\n    T_vals = (np.where(branches[:, 8] > 0, branches[:, 8], 1.0) *\n              np.exp(1j * branches[:, 9] * np.pi / 180))\n    y_vals = 1.0 / (branches[:, 2] + 1j * branches[:, 3])\n    b_vals = branches[:, 4]\n\n    branch_data = []\n    for idx in range(n_branch):\n        if branch_status[idx] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        i, j = fbus[idx], tbus[idx]\n        V_i = vm_val[i] * np.exp(1j * va_val[i] * np.pi / 180)\n        V_j = vm_val[j] * np.exp(1j * va_val[j] * np.pi / 180)\n\n        t = T_vals[idx]\n        y = y_vals[idx]\n        b = b_vals[idx]\n\n        # Shunt admittance\n        y_shunt = 1j * b / 2\n\n        # Power flow from i to j\n        S_ij = (y / (np.conj(t) * t) + y_shunt) * np.conj(V_i) * V_i - \\\n               (y / np.conj(t)) * np.conj(V_i) * V_j\n\n        # Power flow from j to i\n        S_ji = (y + y_shunt) * np.conj(V_j) * V_j - \\\n               (y / t) * np.conj(V_j) * V_i\n\n        flow_from_mva = np.abs(S_ij) * baseMVA\n        flow_to_mva = np.abs(S_ji) * baseMVA\n\n        loading_pct = max(flow_from_mva, flow_to_mva) / limit_mva[idx] * 100\n\n        branch_data.append({\n            \"from_bus\": int(branches[idx, 0]),\n            \"to_bus\": int(branches[idx, 1]),\n            \"loading_pct\": float(loading_pct),\n            \"flow_from_MVA\": float(flow_from_mva),\n            \"flow_to_MVA\": float(flow_to_mva),\n            \"limit_MVA\": float(limit_mva[idx])\n        })\n\n    # Sort by loading and get top 10\n    branch_data.sort(key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n    most_loaded = branch_data[:10]\n\n    # Feasibility check\n    max_p_mismatch = 0\n    max_q_mismatch = 0\n    max_voltage_violation = 0\n    max_branch_overload = 0\n\n    # Check power balance\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        V_i = vm_val[i] * np.exp(1j * va_val[i] * np.pi / 180)\n        p_gen, q_gen = 0, 0\n        for g in range(n_gen):\n            if gen_bus[g] == i:\n                p_gen += pg_val[g]\n                q_gen += qg_val[g]\n\n        p_flow, q_flow = 0, 0\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            if Y[i, j] != 0:\n                V_j = vm_val[j] * np.exp(1j * va_val[j] * np.pi / 180)\n                S_ij = np.conj(V_i) * (Y[i, j] * V_j)\n                p_flow += np.real(S_ij) * baseMVA\n                q_flow += np.imag(S_ij) * baseMVA\n\n        p_mismatch = abs(p_gen - pd_mw[i] - p_flow)\n        q_mismatch = abs(q_gen - qd_mvar[i] - q_flow)\n        max_p_mismatch = max(max_p_mismatch, p_mismatch)\n        max_q_mismatch = max(max_q_mismatch, q_mismatch)\n\n    # Check voltage limits\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        violation = max(0, bus_vm_min[i] - vm_val[i]) + max(0, vm_val[i] - bus_vm_max[i])\n        max_voltage_violation = max(max_voltage_violation, violation)\n\n    # Check branch limits\n    for branch in branch_data:\n        overload = max(0, branch['flow_from_MVA'] - branch['limit_MVA'],\n                      branch['flow_to_MVA'] - branch['limit_MVA'])\n        max_branch_overload = max(max_branch_overload, overload)\n\n    # Build report\n    report = {\n        \"summary\": {\n            \"total_cost_per_hour\": float(cost_val),\n            \"total_load_MW\": float(total_load_mw),\n            \"total_load_MVAr\": float(total_load_mvar),\n            \"total_generation_MW\": float(total_gen_mw),\n            \"total_generation_MVAr\": float(total_gen_mvar),\n            \"total_losses_MW\": float(total_losses_mw),\n            \"solver_status\": status\n        },\n        \"generators\": generators,\n        \"buses\": buses_report,\n        \"most_loaded_branches\": most_loaded,\n        \"feasibility_check\": {\n            \"max_p_mismatch_MW\": float(max_p_mismatch),\n            \"max_q_mismatch_MVAr\": float(max_q_mismatch),\n            \"max_voltage_violation_pu\": float(max_voltage_violation),\n            \"max_branch_overload_MVA\": float(max_branch_overload)\n        }\n    }\n\n    return report\n\ndef main():\n    # Load network\n    print(\"Loading network data...\", file=sys.stderr)\n    network = load_network('/root/network.json')\n\n    # Solve AC OPF\n    print(\"Solving AC OPF...\", file=sys.stderr)\n    sol, opti, status, variables, network_info = solve_ac_opf(network)\n\n    # Generate report\n    print(\"Generating report...\", file=sys.stderr)\n    report = generate_report(sol, opti, status, variables, network_info)\n\n    # Write report\n    with open('/root/report.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(report, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(f\"Report generated: solver_status = {report['summary']['solver_status']}\",\n          file=sys.stderr)\n    print(f\"Total cost: ${report['summary']['total_cost_per_hour']:,.2f}/hour\")\n    print(f\"Total generation: {report['summary']['total_generation_MW']:.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Total load: {report['summary']['total_load_MW']:.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Losses: {report['summary']['total_losses_MW']:.2f} MW\")\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

MATPOWER branch format: [fbus tbus r x b rateA rateB rateC ratio angle status angmin angmax]\n    fbus = branches[:, 0].astype(int) - 1  # Convert to 0-indexing\n    tbus = branches[:, 1].astype(int) - 1\n    r = branches[:, 2]\n    x = branches[:, 3]\n    b = branches[:, 4]\n    ratio = branches[:, 8]  # transformer ratio\n    angle_shift = branches[:, 9] * np.pi / 180  # phase shift in radians\n    status = branches[:, 10]\n\n    # Series impedance and admittance\n    z = r + 1j * x\n    y_series = 1.0 / z\n\n    # Transformer ratios (default 1.0 for lines)\n    T = np.where(ratio > 0, ratio, 1.0) * np.exp(1j * angle_shift)\n\n    # Build full admittance matrix\n    Y = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n\n    for idx in range(len(branches)):\n        if status[idx] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        i, j = fbus[idx], tbus[idx]\n        t = T[idx]\n        y = y_series[idx]\n\n        # Shunt admittance\n        y_shunt_i = 1j * b[idx] / 2\n        y_shunt_j = 1j * b[idx] / 2\n\n        # Off-diagonal elements\n        Y[i, j] -= y / np.conj(t)\n        Y[j, i] -= y / t\n\n        # Diagonal elements\n        Y[i, i] += y / (np.conj(t) * t) + y_shunt_i\n        Y[j, j] += y + y_shunt_j\n\n    # Add bus shunts (none in this dataset but included for completeness)\n    buses = np.array(network['bus'])\n    gs = buses[:, 4]\n    bs = buses[:, 5]\n    Y += np.diag(gs + 1j * bs)\n\n    return Y, fbus, tbus, T, y_series, status\n\ndef solve_ac_opf(network: Dict) -> Tuple[Dict, Dict]:\n    \"\"\"Solve AC OPF using CasADi and IPOPT\n\n    Returns:\n        solution: solver solution with variables\n        info: solver info dict\n    \"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    buses = np.array(network['bus'])\n    gens = np.array(network['gen'])\n    gencost = np.array(network['gencost'])\n    branches = np.array(network['branch'])\n\n    n_bus = len(buses)\n    n_gen = len(gens)\n    n_branch = len(branches)\n\n    # MATPOWER indices (convert to 0-based)\n    bus_i = buses[:, 0].astype(int) - 1\n    bus_type = buses[:, 1].astype(int)  # 1=PQ, 2=PV, 3=ref\n    pd_mw = buses[:, 2]  # Load P\n    qd_mvar = buses[:, 3]  # Load Q\n    vm_max = buses[:, 11]\n    vm_min = buses[:, 12]\n\n    gen_bus = gens[:, 0].astype(int) - 1\n    gen_pg = gens[:, 1]  # Initial P\n    gen_qg = gens[:, 2]  # Initial Q\n    gen_qmax = gens[:, 3]\n    gen_qmin = gens[:, 4]\n    gen_status = gens[:, 7]\n    gen_pmax = gens[:, 8]\n    gen_pmin = gens[:, 9]\n\n    # Cost function: c2*P^2 + c1*P + c0\n    cost_type = gencost[:, 0].astype(int)\n    cost_n = gencost[:, 3].astype(int)\n    c2 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    c1 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    c0 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if cost_type[i] == 2:  # Polynomial cost\n            n = cost_n[i]\n            if n >= 3:\n                c2[i] = gencost[i, 4]\n                c1[i] = gencost[i, 5] if n >= 3 else 0\n                c0[i] = gencost[i, 6] if n >= 3 else 0\n\n    # Build admittance matrix\n    Y, fbus, tbus, T, y_series, branch_status = build_admittance_matrix(network)\n\n    # Branch limits\n    branch_limit = branches[:, 5]  # rateA in MVA\n\n    # Reference bus (slack bus)\n    ref_bus = np.where(bus_type == 3)[0]\n    if len(ref_bus) == 0:\n        ref_bus = np.array([0])\n    else:\n        ref_bus = ref_bus[0]\n\n    # ===== Create Optimization Problem =====\n    opti = ca.Opti()\n\n    # Variables: voltage magnitudes and angles\n    vm = opti.variable(n_bus)  # voltage magnitudes (p.u.)\n    va = opti.variable(n_bus)  # voltage angles (radians)\n\n    # Generator dispatch\n    pg = opti.variable(n_gen)  # real power\n    qg = opti.variable(n_gen)  # reactive power\n\n    # ===== Objective: Minimize generation cost =====\n    cost = ca.sum1(c2 * pg**2 + c1 * pg + c0)\n    opti.minimize(cost)\n\n    # ===== Constraints =====\n\n    # Voltage magnitude bounds\n    opti.subject_to(vm >= vm_min)\n    opti.subject_to(vm <= vm_max)\n\n    # Generator bounds (only active generators)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[i] == 1:\n            opti.subject_to(pg[i] >= gen_pmin[i] / baseMVA)\n            opti.subject_to(pg[i] <= gen_pmax[i] / baseMVA)\n            opti.subject_to(qg[i] >= gen_qmin[i] / baseMVA)\n            opti.subject_to(qg[i] <= gen_qmax[i] / baseMVA)\n        else:\n            opti.subject_to(pg[i] == 0)\n            opti.subject_to(qg[i] == 0)\n\n    # Reference bus angle\n    opti.subject_to(va[ref_bus] == 0)\n\n    # Power flow constraints (nodal balance)\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        # Bus voltage as complex number\n        V_i = vm[i] * ca.exp(1j * va[i])\n\n        # Generation and load\n        p_gen = 0\n        q_gen = 0\n        for g in range(n_gen):\n            if gen_bus[g] == i:\n                p_gen += pg[g]\n                q_gen += qg[g]\n\n        # Convert load from MW/MVAr to p.u.\n        p_load = pd_mw[i] / baseMVA\n        q_load = qd_mvar[i] / baseMVA\n\n        # Power balance: S_gen - S_load = sum of branch flows\n        p_mismatch = 0\n        q_mismatch = 0\n\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            if Y[i, j] != 0:\n                V_j = vm[j] * ca.exp(1j * va[j])\n                S_ij = ca.conj(V_i) * (Y[i, j] * V_j)\n                p_mismatch += ca.real(S_ij)\n                q_mismatch += ca.imag(S_ij)\n\n        opti.subject_to(p_gen - p_load == p_mismatch)\n        opti.subject_to(q_gen - q_load == q_mismatch)\n\n    # Branch flow limits (apparent power)\n    for idx in range(n_branch):\n        if branch_status[idx] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        i, j = fbus[idx], tbus[idx]\n        t = T[idx]\n        y = y_series[idx]\n        limit = branch_limit[idx] / baseMVA\n\n        V_i = vm[i] * ca.exp(1j * va[i])\n        V_j = vm[j] * ca.exp(1j * va[j])\n\n        # Power flow from i to j\n        S_ij = (y / (ca.conj(t) * t) + 1j * np.imag(Y[i, i])) * ca.conj(V_i) * V_i - \\\n               (y / ca.conj(t)) * ca.conj(V_i) * V_j\n\n        # Power flow from j to i\n        S_ji = (y + 1j * np.imag(Y[j, j])) * ca.conj(V_j) * V_j - \\\n               (y / t) * ca.conj(V_j) * V_i\n\n        # Apparent power limits\n        opti.subject_to(ca.sqrt(ca.real(S_ij)**2 + ca.imag(S_ij)**2) <= limit)\n        opti.subject_to(ca.sqrt(ca.real(S_ji)**2 + ca.imag(S_ji)**2) <= limit)\n\n    # ===== Initial Guess =====\n    opti.set_initial(vm, 1.0)\n    opti.set_initial(va, 0.0)\n    opti.set_initial(pg, gen_pg / baseMVA)\n    opti.set_initial(qg, gen_qg / baseMVA)\n\n    # ===== Solver Setup =====\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt': {\n            'print_level': 0,\n            'max_iter': 3000,\n            'tol': 1e-6,\n            'acceptable_tol': 1e-4\n        }\n    }\n    opti.solver('ipopt', opts)\n\n    # ===== Solve =====\n    try:\n        sol = opti.solve()\n        status = 'optimal' if sol.stats()['return_status'] == 'Solve_Succeeded' else 'suboptimal'\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Solver failed: {e}\", file=sys.stderr)\n        sol = opti.debug\n        status = 'failed'\n\n    return sol, opti, status, (vm, va, pg, qg), (buses, gens, branches, Y, baseMVA)\n\ndef generate_report(sol, opti, status, variables, network_info) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Generate report.json from solver solution\"\"\"\n    vm, va, pg, qg = variables\n    buses, gens, branches, Y, baseMVA = network_info\n\n    # Extract solution values\n    vm_val = np.array(sol.value(vm))\n    va_val = np.array(sol.value(va)) * 180 / np.pi  # convert to degrees\n    pg_val = np.array(sol.value(pg)) * baseMVA  # convert to MW\n    qg_val = np.array(sol.value(qg)) * baseMVA  # convert to MVAr\n    cost_val = float(sol.value(opti.f))\n\n    n_gen = len(gens)\n    n_bus = len(buses)\n    n_branch = len(branches)\n\n    gen_bus = gens[:, 0].astype(int) - 1\n    gen_status = gens[:, 7]\n    gen_qmax = gens[:, 3]\n    gen_qmin = gens[:, 4]\n    gen_pmax = gens[:, 8]\n    gen_pmin = gens[:, 9]\n\n    bus_i = buses[:, 0].astype(int)\n    bus_vm_max = buses[:, 11]\n    bus_vm_min = buses[:, 12]\n\n    pd_mw = buses[:, 2]\n    qd_mvar = buses[:, 3]\n    total_load_mw = np.sum(pd_mw)\n    total_load_mvar = np.sum(qd_mvar)\n\n    # Calculate total generation and losses\n    total_gen_mw = np.sum(pg_val[gen_status == 1])\n    total_gen_mvar = np.sum(qg_val[gen_status == 1])\n    total_losses_mw = total_gen_mw - total_load_mw\n\n    # Build generators report\n    generators = []\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[i] == 1:\n            generators.append({\n                \"id\": int(i + 1),\n                \"bus\": int(gens[i, 0]),\n                \"pg_MW\": float(pg_val[i]),\n                \"qg_MVAr\": float(qg_val[i]),\n                \"pmin_MW\": float(gen_pmin[i]),\n                \"pmax_MW\": float(gen_pmax[i]),\n                \"qmin_MVAr\": float(gen_qmin[i]),\n                \"qmax_MVAr\": float(gen_qmax[i])\n            })\n\n    # Build buses report\n    buses_report = []\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        buses_report.append({\n            \"id\": int(bus_i[i]),\n            \"vm_pu\": float(vm_val[i]),\n            \"va_deg\": float(va_val[i]),\n            \"vmin_pu\": float(bus_vm_min[i]),\n            \"vmax_pu\": float(bus_vm_max[i])\n        })\n\n    # Calculate branch flows and find most loaded\n    branch_status = branches[:, 10]\n    fbus = branches[:, 0].astype(int) - 1\n    tbus = branches[:, 1].astype(int) - 1\n    limit_mva = branches[:, 5]  # rateA\n\n    T_vals = (np.where(branches[:, 8] > 0, branches[:, 8], 1.0) *\n              np.exp(1j * branches[:, 9] * np.pi / 180))\n    y_vals = 1.0 / (branches[:, 2] + 1j * branches[:, 3])\n    b_vals = branches[:, 4]\n\n    branch_data = []\n    for idx in range(n_branch):\n        if branch_status[idx] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        i, j = fbus[idx], tbus[idx]\n        V_i = vm_val[i] * np.exp(1j * va_val[i] * np.pi / 180)\n        V_j = vm_val[j] * np.exp(1j * va_val[j] * np.pi / 180)\n\n        t = T_vals[idx]\n        y = y_vals[idx]\n        b = b_vals[idx]\n\n        # Shunt admittance\n        y_shunt = 1j * b / 2\n\n        # Power flow from i to j\n        S_ij = (y / (np.conj(t) * t) + y_shunt) * np.conj(V_i) * V_i - \\\n               (y / np.conj(t)) * np.conj(V_i) * V_j\n\n        # Power flow from j to i\n        S_ji = (y + y_shunt) * np.conj(V_j) * V_j - \\\n               (y / t) * np.conj(V_j) * V_i\n\n        flow_from_mva = np.abs(S_ij) * baseMVA\n        flow_to_mva = np.abs(S_ji) * baseMVA\n\n        loading_pct = max(flow_from_mva, flow_to_mva) / limit_mva[idx] * 100\n\n        branch_data.append({\n            \"from_bus\": int(branches[idx, 0]),\n            \"to_bus\": int(branches[idx, 1]),\n            \"loading_pct\": float(loading_pct),\n            \"flow_from_MVA\": float(flow_from_mva),\n            \"flow_to_MVA\": float(flow_to_mva),\n            \"limit_MVA\": float(limit_mva[idx])\n        })\n\n    # Sort by loading and get top 10\n    branch_data.sort(key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n    most_loaded = branch_data[:10]\n\n    # Feasibility check\n    max_p_mismatch = 0\n    max_q_mismatch = 0\n    max_voltage_violation = 0\n    max_branch_overload = 0\n\n    # Check power balance\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        V_i = vm_val[i] * np.exp(1j * va_val[i] * np.pi / 180)\n        p_gen, q_gen = 0, 0\n        for g in range(n_gen):\n            if gen_bus[g] == i:\n                p_gen += pg_val[g]\n                q_gen += qg_val[g]\n\n        p_flow, q_flow = 0, 0\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            if Y[i, j] != 0:\n                V_j = vm_val[j] * np.exp(1j * va_val[j] * np.pi / 180)\n                S_ij = np.conj(V_i) * (Y[i, j] * V_j)\n                p_flow += np.real(S_ij) * baseMVA\n                q_flow += np.imag(S_ij) * baseMVA\n\n        p_mismatch = abs(p_gen - pd_mw[i] - p_flow)\n        q_mismatch = abs(q_gen - qd_mvar[i] - q_flow)\n        max_p_mismatch = max(max_p_mismatch, p_mismatch)\n        max_q_mismatch = max(max_q_mismatch, q_mismatch)\n\n    # Check voltage limits\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        violation = max(0, bus_vm_min[i] - vm_val[i]) + max(0, vm_val[i] - bus_vm_max[i])\n        max_voltage_violation = max(max_voltage_violation, violation)\n\n    # Check branch limits\n    for branch in branch_data:\n        overload = max(0, branch['flow_from_MVA'] - branch['limit_MVA'],\n                      branch['flow_to_MVA'] - branch['limit_MVA'])\n        max_branch_overload = max(max_branch_overload, overload)\n\n    # {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /logs/agent/sessions/skills/casadi-ipopt-nlp\n\n# CasADi + IPOPT for Nonlinear Programming\n\nCasADi is a symbolic framework for nonlinear optimization. IPOPT is an interior-point solver for large-scale NLP.\n\n## Quick start (Linux)\n\n```bash\napt-get update -qq && apt-get install -y -qq libgfortran5\npip install numpy==1.26.4 casadi==3.6.7\n```\n\n## Building an NLP\n\n### 1. Decision variables\n\n```python\nimport casadi as ca\n\nn_bus, n_gen = 100, 20\nVm = ca.MX.sym(\"Vm\", n_bus)   # Voltage magnitudes\nVa = ca.MX.sym(\"Va\", n_bus)   # Voltage angles (radians)\nPg = ca.MX.sym(\"Pg\", n_gen)   # Real power\nQg = ca.MX.sym(\"Qg\", n_gen)   # Reactive power\n\n# Stack into single vector for solver\nx = ca.vertcat(Vm, Va, Pg, Qg)\n```\n\n### 2. Objective function\n\nBuild symbolic expression:\n\n```python\n# Quadratic cost: sum of c2*P^2 + c1*P + c0\nobj = ca.MX(0)\nfor k in range(n_gen):\n    obj += c2[k] * Pg[k]**2 + c1[k] * Pg[k] + c0[k]\n```\n\n### 3. Constraints\n\nCollect constraints in lists with bounds:\n\n```python\ng_expr = []  # Constraint expressions\nlbg = []     # Lower bounds\nubg = []     # Upper bounds\n\n# Equality constraint: g(x) = 0\ng_expr.append(some_expression)\nlbg.append(0.0)\nubg.append(0.0)\n\n# Inequality constraint: g(x) <= limit\ng_expr.append(another_expression)\nlbg.append(-ca.inf)\nubg.append(limit)\n\n# Two-sided: lo <= g(x) <= hi\ng_expr.append(bounded_expression)\nlbg.append(lo)\nubg.append(hi)\n\ng = ca.vertcat(*g_expr)\n```\n\n### 4. Variable bounds\n\n```python\n# Stack bounds matching variable order\nlbx = np.concatenate([Vm_min, Va_min, Pg_min, Qg_min]).tolist()\nubx = np.concatenate([Vm_max, Va_max, Pg_max, Qg_max]).tolist()\n```\n\n### 5. Create and call solver\n\n```python\nnlp = {\"x\": x, \"f\": obj, \"g\": g}\nopts = {\n    \"ipopt.print_level\": 0,\n    \"ipopt.max_iter\": 2000,\n    \"ipopt.tol\": 1e-7,\n    \"ipopt.acceptable_tol\": 1e-5,\n    \"ipopt.mu_strategy\": \"adaptive\",\n    \"print_time\": False,\n}\nsolver = ca.nlpsol(\"solver\", \"ipopt\", nlp, opts)\n\nsol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg, ubg=ubg)\nx_opt = np.array(sol[\"x\"]).flatten()\nobj_val = float(sol[\"f\"])\n```\n\n## IPOPT options (tuning guide)\n\n| Option | Default | Recommendation | Notes |\n|--------|---------|----------------|-------|\n| `tol` | 1e-8 | 1e-7 | Convergence tolerance |\n| `acceptable_tol` | 1e-6 | 1e-5 | Fallback if tol not reached |\n| `max_iter` | 3000 | 2000 | Increase for hard problems |\n| `mu_strategy` | monotone | adaptive | Better for nonconvex |\n| `print_level` | 5 | 0 | Quiet output |\n\n## Initialization matters\n\nNonlinear solvers are sensitive to starting points. Use multiple initializations:\n\n```python\ninitializations = [x0_from_data, x0_flat_start]\nbest_sol = None\n\nfor x0 in initializations:\n    try:\n        sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg, ubg=ubg)\n        if best_sol is None or float(sol[\"f\"]) < float(best_sol[\"f\"]):\n            best_sol = sol\n    except Exception:\n        continue\n\nif best_sol is None:\n    raise RuntimeError(\"Solver failed from all initializations\")\n```\n\n**Good initialization strategies:**\n- Data-derived: Use values from input data, clipped to bounds\n- Flat start: Nominal values (e.g., Vm=1.0, Va=0.0)\n- Always enforce known constraints in initial point (e.g., reference angle = 0)\n\n## Extracting solutions\n\n```python\nx_opt = np.array(sol[\"x\"]).flatten()\n\n# Unpack by slicing (must match variable order)\nVm_sol = x_opt[:n_bus]\nVa_sol = x_opt[n_bus:2*n_bus]\nPg_sol = x_opt[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen]\nQg_sol = x_opt[2*n_bus+n_gen:]\n```\n\n## Power systems patterns\n\n### Per-unit scaling\n\nWork in per-unit internally, convert for output:\n\n```python\nbaseMVA = 100.0\nPg_pu = Pg_MW / baseMVA      # Input conversion\nPg_MW = Pg_pu * baseMVA      # Output conversion\n```\n\nCost functions often expect MW, not per-unit - check the formulation.\n\n### Bus ID mapping\n\nPower system bus numbers may not be contiguous:\n\n```python\nbus_id_to_idx = {int(bus[i, 0]): i for i in range(n_bus)}\ngen_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[int(gen_row[0])]\n```\n\n### Aggregating per-bus quantities\n\n```python\nPg_bus = [ca.MX(0) for _ in range(n_bus)]\nfor k in range(n_gen):\n    bus_idx = gen_bus_idx[k]\n    Pg_bus[bus_idx] += Pg[k]\n```\n\n## Common failure modes\n\n- **Infeasible**: Check bound consistency, constraint signs, unit conversions\n- **Slow convergence**: Try different initialization, relax tolerances temporarily\n- **Wrong tap handling**: MATPOWER uses `tap=0` to mean 1.0, not zero\n- **Angle units**: Data often in degrees, solver needs radians\n- **Shunt signs**: Check convention for Gs (conductance) vs Bs (susceptance)\n- **Over-rounding outputs**: Keep high precision (≥6 decimals) in results\n"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ee8c4e16-377d-45bd-a75b-0a5876924dc7","uuid":"093d0195-46ad-417f-923c-d97be0a2ec40","isSynthetic":true}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_vrtx_01JQT7yZn625voEkNuAR6e8z","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01BJaj7vk331Sztm1dDSuZXM","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/ac_opf_solver.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nAC Optimal Power Flow Solver for ISO Base Case Planning\nUses CasADi and IPOPT to find least-cost AC-feasible operating point\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nimport casadi as ca\nfrom typing import Dict, Tuple\nimport sys\n\ndef load_network(filepath: str) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Load MATPOWER format network data\"\"\"\n    with open(filepath, 'r') as f:\n        return json.load(f)\n\ndef build_admittance_matrix(network: Dict) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray, np.ndarray,\n                                                     np.ndarray, np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:\n    \"\"\"Build admittance matrix from branch data\n\n    Returns:\n        Y: complex admittance matrix\n        y_ser: series admittances for each branch\n        T_ratio: transformer ratios for each branch\n        y_shunt_i, y_shunt_j: shunt admittances at each end\n        branch_status: status of each branch\n    \"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    branches = np.array(network['branch'])\n    n_bus = len(network['bus'])\n\n    # MATPOWER branch format: [fbus tbus r x b rateA rateB rateC ratio angle status angmin angmax]\n    fbus = branches[:, 0].astype(int) - 1  # Convert to 0-indexing\n    tbus = branches[:, 1].astype(int) - 1\n    r = branches[:, 2]\n    x = branches[:, 3]\n    b = branches[:, 4]\n    ratio = branches[:, 8]  # transformer ratio\n    angle_shift = branches[:, 9] * np.pi / 180  # phase shift in radians\n    status = branches[:, 10]\n\n    # Series impedance and admittance\n    z = r + 1j * x\n    y_series = np.where(z != 0, 1.0 / z, 0)\n\n    # Transformer ratios (default 1.0 for lines)\n    T = np.where(ratio > 0, ratio, 1.0) * np.exp(1j * angle_shift)\n\n    # Shunt admittance at each bus\n    y_shunt_i = 1j * b / 2\n    y_shunt_j = 1j * b / 2\n\n    # Build full admittance matrix\n    Y = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n\n    for idx in range(len(branches)):\n        if status[idx] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        i, j = fbus[idx], tbus[idx]\n        t = T[idx]\n        y = y_series[idx]\n\n        # Off-diagonal elements\n        Y[i, j] -= y / np.conj(t)\n        Y[j, i] -= y / t\n\n        # Diagonal elements\n        Y[i, i] += y / (np.conj(t) * t) + y_shunt_i[idx]\n        Y[j, j] += y + y_shunt_j[idx]\n\n    # Add bus shunts\n    buses = np.array(network['bus'])\n    gs = buses[:, 4]\n    bs = buses[:, 5]\n    Y += np.diag(gs + 1j * bs)\n\n    return Y, y_series, T, y_shunt_i, y_shunt_j, status\n\ndef solve_ac_opf(network: Dict) -> Tuple[Dict, str]:\n    \"\"\"Solve AC OPF using CasADi and IPOPT\n\n    Returns:\n        sol: solver solution with all variables\n        status: solver status string\n    \"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    buses = np.array(network['bus'])\n    gens = np.array(network['gen'])\n    gencost = np.array(network['gencost'])\n    branches = np.array(network['branch'])\n\n    n_bus = len(buses)\n    n_gen = len(gens)\n    n_branch = len(branches)\n\n    # MATPOWER indices (convert to 0-based)\n    bus_type = buses[:, 1].astype(int)  # 1=PQ, 2=PV, 3=ref\n    pd_mw = buses[:, 2]  # Load P\n    qd_mvar = buses[:, 3]  # Load Q\n    vm_max = buses[:, 11]\n    vm_min = buses[:, 12]\n\n    gen_bus = gens[:, 0].astype(int) - 1\n    gen_pg = gens[:, 1]  # Initial P\n    gen_qg = gens[:, 2]  # Initial Q\n    gen_qmax = gens[:, 3]\n    gen_qmin = gens[:, 4]\n    gen_status = gens[:, 7]\n    gen_pmax = gens[:, 8]\n    gen_pmin = gens[:, 9]\n\n    # Cost function: c2*P^2 + c1*P + c0\n    cost_type = gencost[:, 0].astype(int)\n    cost_n = gencost[:, 3].astype(int)\n    c2 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    c1 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    c0 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if cost_type[i] == 2:  # Polynomial cost\n            n = cost_n[i]\n            if n >= 3:\n                c2[i] = gencost[i, 4]\n                c1[i] = gencost[i, 5] if n >= 3 else 0\n                c0[i] = gencost[i, 6] if n >= 3 else 0\n\n    # Build admittance matrix\n    Y, y_series, T, y_shunt_i, y_shunt_j, branch_status = build_admittance_matrix(network)\n\n    # Branch data\n    fbus = branches[:, 0].astype(int) - 1\n    tbus = branches[:, 1].astype(int) - 1\n    branch_limit = branches[:, 5]  # rateA in MVA\n\n    # Reference bus (slack bus)\n    ref_bus = np.where(bus_type == 3)[0]\n    if len(ref_bus) == 0:\n        ref_bus = 0\n    else:\n        ref_bus = ref_bus[0]\n\n    # ===== Decision Variables =====\n    Vm = ca.MX.sym(\"Vm\", n_bus)  # voltage magnitudes (p.u.)\n    Va = ca.MX.sym(\"Va\", n_bus)  # voltage angles (radians)\n    Pg = ca.MX.sym(\"Pg\", n_gen)  # real power (p.u.)\n    Qg = ca.MX.sym(\"Qg\", n_gen)  # reactive power (p.u.)\n\n    x = ca.vertcat(Vm, Va, Pg, Qg)\n\n    # ===== Objective: Minimize generation cost =====\n    cost = ca.MX(0)\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        Pg_mw = Pg[k] * baseMVA\n        cost += c2[k] * Pg_mw**2 + c1[k] * Pg_mw + c0[k]\n\n    # ===== Constraints =====\n    g_expr = []\n    lbg = []\n    ubg = []\n\n    # Power flow equations (nodal balance)\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        # Bus voltage as complex number\n        V_i = Vm[i] * ca.cos(Va[i]) + 1j * Vm[i] * ca.sin(Va[i])\n\n        # Generation and load\n        p_gen = ca.MX(0)\n        q_gen = ca.MX(0)\n        for g in range(n_gen):\n            if gen_bus[g] == i:\n                p_gen += Pg[g]\n                q_gen += Qg[g]\n\n        # Convert load from MW/MVAr to p.u.\n        p_load = pd_mw[i] / baseMVA\n        q_load = qd_mvar[i] / baseMVA\n\n        # Power balance: sum of branch flows = injection - load\n        p_flow = ca.MX(0)\n        q_flow = ca.MX(0)\n\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            if Y[i, j] != 0 or i == j:\n                V_j = Vm[j] * ca.cos(Va[j]) + 1j * Vm[j] * ca.sin(Va[j])\n\n                # Current from i to j: I_ij = (V_i - V_j) * Y_ij\n                # Power: S_ij = V_i * conj(I_ij)\n                Y_ij = Y[i, j]\n                S_ij = V_i * ca.conj(Y_ij * V_j)\n\n                p_flow += ca.real(S_ij)\n                q_flow += ca.imag(S_ij)\n\n        # Power balance equations\n        g_expr.append(p_gen - p_load - p_flow)\n        lbg.append(0.0)\n        ubg.append(0.0)\n\n        g_expr.append(q_gen - q_load - q_flow)\n        lbg.append(0.0)\n        ubg.append(0.0)\n\n    # Reference bus angle = 0\n    g_expr.append(Va[ref_bus])\n    lbg.append(0.0)\n    ubg.append(0.0)\n\n    g = ca.vertcat(*g_expr)\n\n    # ===== Variable Bounds =====\n    lbx = []\n    ubx = []\n\n    # Voltage magnitude bounds\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        lbx.append(vm_min[i])\n        ubx.append(vm_max[i])\n\n    # Voltage angle bounds (allow -pi to pi)\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        lbx.append(-np.pi)\n        ubx.append(np.pi)\n\n    # Generator real power bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[i] == 1:\n            lbx.append(gen_pmin[i] / baseMVA)\n            ubx.append(gen_pmax[i] / baseMVA)\n        else:\n            lbx.append(0.0)\n            ubx.append(0.0)\n\n    # Generator reactive power bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[i] == 1:\n            lbx.append(gen_qmin[i] / baseMVA)\n            ubx.append(gen_qmax[i] / baseMVA)\n        else:\n            lbx.append(0.0)\n            ubx.append(0.0)\n\n    # ===== Initial Guess =====\n    x0 = []\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        x0.append(1.0)  # Vm\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        x0.append(0.0)  # Va\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        x0.append(gen_pg[i] / baseMVA)  # Pg\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        x0.append(gen_qg[i] / baseMVA)  # Qg\n\n    # Enforce reference bus angle = 0\n    x0[n_bus + ref_bus] = 0.0\n\n    # ===== Solver Setup =====\n    nlp = {\"x\": x, \"f\": cost, \"g\": g}\n    opts = {\n        \"ipopt.print_level\": 0,\n        \"ipopt.max_iter\": 3000,\n        \"ipopt.tol\": 1e-6,\n        \"ipopt.acceptable_tol\": 1e-4,\n        \"ipopt.mu_strategy\": \"adaptive\",\n        \"print_time\": False,\n    }\n\n    try:\n        solver = ca.nlpsol(\"solver\", \"ipopt\", nlp, opts)\n        sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg, ubg=ubg)\n\n        # Check solver status\n        stats = solver.stats()\n        if stats['return_status'] == 'Solve_Succeeded':\n            status = 'optimal'\n        else:\n            status = 'suboptimal'\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Solver error: {e}\", file=sys.stderr)\n        status = 'failed'\n        sol = None\n\n    return sol, status, (Vm, Va, Pg, Qg), (buses, gens, branches, Y, baseMVA,\n                                            fbus, tbus, branch_status, branch_limit,\n                                            gen_bus, gen_status, pd_mw, qd_mvar)\n\ndef generate_report(sol, status, variables, network_info) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Generate report.json from solver solution\"\"\"\n    if sol is None:\n        return {\"error\": \"Solver failed\"}\n\n    Vm, Va, Pg, Qg = variables\n    (buses, gens, branches, Y, baseMVA,\n     fbus, tbus, branch_status, branch_limit,\n     gen_bus, gen_status, pd_mw, qd_mvar) = network_info\n\n    # Extract solution values\n    x_opt = np.array(sol[\"x\"]).flatten()\n\n    n_bus = len(buses)\n    n_gen = len(gens)\n    n_branch = len(branches)\n\n    vm_val = x_opt[:n_bus]\n    va_val = x_opt[n_bus:2*n_bus] * 180 / np.pi  # convert to degrees\n    pg_val = x_opt[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen] * baseMVA  # convert to MW\n    qg_val = x_opt[2*n_bus+n_gen:] * baseMVA  # convert to MVAr\n    cost_val = float(sol[\"f\"])\n\n    gen_qmax = gens[:, 3]\n    gen_qmin = gens[:, 4]\n    gen_pmax = gens[:, 8]\n    gen_pmin = gens[:, 9]\n\n    bus_i = buses[:, 0].astype(int)\n    bus_vm_max = buses[:, 11]\n    bus_vm_min = buses[:, 12]\n\n    total_load_mw = np.sum(pd_mw)\n    total_load_mvar = np.sum(qd_mvar)\n\n    # Calculate total generation and losses\n    total_gen_mw = np.sum(pg_val[gen_status == 1])\n    total_gen_mvar = np.sum(qg_val[gen_status == 1])\n    total_losses_mw = total_gen_mw - total_load_mw\n\n    # Build generators report\n    generators = []\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[i] == 1:\n            generators.append({\n                \"id\": int(i + 1),\n                \"bus\": int(gens[i, 0]),\n                \"pg_MW\": float(pg_val[i]),\n                \"qg_MVAr\": float(qg_val[i]),\n                \"pmin_MW\": float(gen_pmin[i]),\n                \"pmax_MW\": float(gen_pmax[i]),\n                \"qmin_MVAr\": float(gen_qmin[i]),\n                \"qmax_MVAr\": float(gen_qmax[i])\n            })\n\n    # Build buses report\n    buses_report = []\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        buses_report.append({\n            \"id\": int(bus_i[i]),\n            \"vm_pu\": float(vm_val[i]),\n            \"va_deg\": float(va_val[i]),\n            \"vmin_pu\": float(bus_vm_min[i]),\n            \"vmax_pu\": float(bus_vm_max[i])\n        })\n\n    # Calculate branch flows and find most loaded\n    branch_data = []\n    for idx in range(n_branch):\n        if branch_status[idx] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        i, j = fbus[idx], tbus[idx]\n        V_i = vm_val[i] * np.exp(1j * va_val[i] * np.pi / 180)\n        V_j = vm_val[j] * np.exp(1j * va_val[j] * np.pi / 180)\n\n        Y_ij = Y[i, j]\n        Y_ji = Y[j, i]\n\n        # Power flow from i to j\n        S_ij = V_i * np.conj(Y_ij * V_j)\n        flow_from_mva = np.abs(S_ij) * baseMVA\n\n        # Power flow from j to i\n        S_ji = V_j * np.conj(Y_ji * V_i)\n        flow_to_mva = np.abs(S_ji) * baseMVA\n\n        limit = branch_limit[idx]\n        loading_pct = max(flow_from_mva, flow_to_mva) / limit * 100\n\n        branch_data.append({\n            \"from_bus\": int(branches[idx, 0]),\n            \"to_bus\": int(branches[idx, 1]),\n            \"loading_pct\": float(loading_pct),\n            \"flow_from_MVA\": float(flow_from_mva),\n            \"flow_to_MVA\": float(flow_to_mva),\n            \"limit_MVA\": float(limit)\n        })\n\n    # Sort by loading and get top 10\n    branch_data.sort(key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n    most_loaded = branch_data[:10]\n\n    # Feasibility check\n    max_p_mismatch = 0\n    max_q_mismatch = 0\n    max_voltage_violation = 0\n    max_branch_overload = 0\n\n    # Check power balance\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        V_i = vm_val[i] * np.exp(1j * va_val[i] * np.pi / 180)\n        p_gen, q_gen = 0, 0\n        for g in range(n_gen):\n            if gen_bus[g] == i:\n                p_gen += pg_val[g]\n                q_gen += qg_val[g]\n\n        p_flow, q_flow = 0, 0\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            if Y[i, j] != 0:\n                V_j = vm_val[j] * np.exp(1j * va_val[j] * np.pi / 180)\n                S_ij = V_i * np.conj(Y[i, j] * V_j)\n                p_flow += np.real(S_ij) * baseMVA\n                q_flow += np.imag(S_ij) * baseMVA\n\n        p_mismatch = abs(p_gen - pd_mw[i] - p_flow)\n        q_mismatch = abs(q_gen - qd_mvar[i] - q_flow)\n        max_p_mismatch = max(max_p_mismatch, p_mismatch)\n        max_q_mismatch = max(max_q_mismatch, q_mismatch)\n\n    # Check voltage limits\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        violation = max(0, bus_vm_min[i] - vm_val[i]) + max(0, vm_val[i] - bus_vm_max[i])\n        max_voltage_violation = max(max_voltage_violation, violation)\n\n    # Check branch limits\n    for branch in branch_data:\n        overload = max(0, branch['flow_from_MVA'] - branch['limit_MVA'],\n                      branch['flow_to_MVA'] - branch['limit_MVA'])\n        max_branch_overload = max(max_branch_overload, overload)\n\n    # \"\"\"Build admittance matrix from branch data\n\n    Returns:\n        Y: complex admittance matrix\n        y_ser: series admittances for each branch\n        T_ratio: transformer ratios for each np.array(network['branch'])\n    n_bus = len(network['bus'])\n\n    # MATPOWER branch format: [fbus tbus r x b rateA rateB rateC ratio angle status angmin angmax]\n    fbus = branches[:, 0].astype(int) - 1  # Convert to 0-indexing\n    tbus = branches[:, 1].astype(int) - 1\n    r = branches[:, 2]\n    x = branches[:, 3]\n    b = branches[:, 4]\n    ratio = branches[:, 8]  # transformer ratio\n    angle_shift = branches[:, 9] * np.pi / 180  # phase shift in radians\n    status = branches[:, 10]\n\n    # Series impedance and admittance\n    z = r + 1j * x\n    y_series = np.where(z != 0, 1.0 / z, 0)\n\n    # Transformer ratios (default 1.0 for lines)\n    T = np.where(ratio > 0, ratio, 1.0) * np.exp(1j * angle_shift)\n\n    # Shunt admittance at each bus\n    y_shunt_i = 1j * b / 2\n    y_shunt_j = 1j * b / 2\n\n    # Build full admittance matrix\n    Y = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n\n    for idx in range(len(branches)):\n        if     Y[j, i] -= y / t\n\n        # Diagonal elements\n        Y[i, i] += y / (np.conj(t) * t) + y_shunt_i[idx]\n        Y[j, j] += y + y_shunt_j[idx]\n\n    # Add bus shunts\n    buses = + c1[k] * Pg_mw + c0[k]\n\n    # ===== Constraints =====\n    g_expr = []\n    lbg = []\n    ubg = []\n\n    # Power flow equations (nodal balance)\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        # Bus voltage as complex number\n        V_i = Vm[i] * ca.cos(Va[i]) + 1j * Vm[i] * ca.sin(Va[i])\n\n        # Generation and load\n        p_gen = ca.MX(0)\n        q_gen = ca.MX(0)\n        for g in      ubg.append(0.0)\n\n    # Reference bus angle = 0\n    g_expr.append(Va[ref_bus])\n    lbg.append(0.0)\n    ubg.append(0.0)\n\n    g = ca.vertcat(*g_expr)\n\n    # ===== Variable Bounds =====\n    lbx = []\n    ubx = []\n\n    # Voltage magnitude bounds\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        lbx.append(vm_min[i])\n        ubx.append(vm_max[i])\n\n    # Voltage angle bounds (allow -pi to pi)\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        lbx.append(-np.pi)\n        ubx.append(np.pi)\n\n    # Generator real power bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[i] == 1:\n            lbx.append(gen_pmin[i] / baseMVA)\n            ubx.append(gen_pmax[i] / baseMVA)\n        else:\n            lbx.append(0.0)\n            ubx.append(0.0)\n\n    # Generator reactive power bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[i] == 1:\n            lbx.append(gen_qmin[i] / baseMVA)\n            ubx.append(gen_qmax[i] / baseMVA)\n        else:\n            lbx.append(0.0)\n            ubx.append(0.0)\n\n    # ===== Initial Guess =====\n    x0 = []\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        x0.append(1.0)  # Vm\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        x0.append(0.0)  # Va\n    for i in        \"ipopt.acceptable_tol\": 1e-4,\n        \"ipopt.mu_strategy\": \"adaptive\",\n        \"print_time\": False,\n    }\n\n    try:\n        solver = ca.nlpsol(\"solver\", \"ipopt\", nlp, opts)\n point\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nimport casadi as ca\nfrom typing import Dict, List, Tuple\nimport sys\n\ndef load_network(filepath: str) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Load MATPOWER format network data\"\"\"\n    with open(filepath, 'r') as f:\n        return json.load(f)\n\ndef build_admittance_matrix(network: Dict, baseMVA: float) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:\n    \"\"\"Build admittance matrix from branch data\n\n    Returns:\n        Y: complex admittance matrix (sparse representation)\n        Yf, Yt: admittance matrices for from/to buses\n    \"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    branches = np.array(network['branch'])\n    n_bus = len(network['bus'])\n\n    # MATPOWER branch format: [fbus tbus r x b rateA rateB rateC ratio angle status angmin angmax]\n    fbus = branches[:, 0].astype(int) - 1  # Convert to 0-indexing\n    tbus = branches[:, 1].astype(int) - 1\n    r = branches[:, 2]\n    x = branches[:, 3]\n    b = branches[:, 4]\n    ratio = branches[:, 8]  # transformer ratio\n    angle_shift = branches[:, 9] * np.pi / 180  # phase shift in radians\n    status = branches[:, 10]\n\n    # Series impedance and admittance\n    z = r + 1j * x\n    y_series = 1.0 / z\n\n    # Transformer ratios (default 1.0 for lines)\n    T = np.where(ratio > 0, ratio, 1.0) * np.exp(1j * angle_shift)\n\n    # Build full admittance matrix\n    Y = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n\n    for idx in range(len(branches)):\n        if status[idx] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        i, j = fbus[idx], tbus[idx]\n        t = T[idx]\n        y = y_series[idx]\n\n        # Shunt admittance\n        y_shunt_i = 1j * b[idx] / 2\n        y_shunt_j = 1j * b[idx] / 2\n\n        # Off-diagonal elements\n        Y[i, j] -= y / np.conj(t)\n        Y[j, i] -= y / t\n\n        # Diagonal elements\n        Y[i, i] += y / (np.conj(t) * t) + y_shunt_i\n        Y[j, j] += y + y_shunt_j\n\n    # Add bus shunts (none in this dataset but included for completeness)\n    buses = np.array(network['bus'])\n    gs = buses[:, 4]\n    bs = buses[:, 5]\n    Y += np.diag(gs + 1j * bs)\n\n    return Y, fbus, tbus, T, y_series, status\n\ndef solve_ac_opf(network: Dict) -> Tuple[Dict, Dict]:\n    \"\"\"Solve AC OPF using CasADi and IPOPT\n\n    Returns:\n        solution: solver solution with variables\n        info: solver info dict\n    \"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    buses = np.array(network['bus'])\n    gens = np.array(network['gen'])\n    gencost = np.array(network['gencost'])\n    branches = np.array(network['branch'])\n\n    n_bus = len(buses)\n    n_gen = len(gens)\n    n_branch = len(branches)\n\n    # MATPOWER indices (convert to 0-based)\n    bus_i = buses[:, 0].astype(int) - 1\n    bus_type = buses[:, 1].astype(int)  # 1=PQ, 2=PV, 3=ref\n    pd_mw = buses[:, 2]  # Load P\n    qd_mvar = buses[:, 3]  # Load Q\n    vm_max = buses[:, 11]\n    vm_min = buses[:, 12]\n\n    gen_bus = gens[:, 0].astype(int) - 1\n    gen_pg = gens[:, 1]  # Initial P\n    gen_qg = gens[:, 2]  # Initial Q\n    gen_qmax = gens[:, 3]\n    gen_qmin = gens[:, 4]\n    gen_status = gens[:, 7]\n    gen_pmax = gens[:, 8]\n    gen_pmin = gens[:, 9]\n\n    # Cost function: c2*P^2 + c1*P + c0\n    cost_type = gencost[:, 0].astype(int)\n    cost_n = gencost[:, 3].astype(int)\n    c2 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    c1 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    c0 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if cost_type[i] == 2:  # Polynomial cost\n            n = cost_n[i]\n            if n >= 3:\n                c2[i] = gencost[i, 4]\n                c1[i] = gencost[i, 5] if n >= 3 else 0\n                c0[i] = gencost[i, 6] if n >= 3 else 0\n\n    # Build admittance matrix\n    Y, fbus, tbus, T, y_series, branch_status = build_admittance_matrix(network, baseMVA)\n\n    # Branch limits\n    branch_limit = branches[:, 5]  # rateA in MVA\n\n    # Reference bus (slack bus)\n    ref_bus = np.where(bus_type == 3)[0]\n    if len(ref_bus) == 0:\n        ref_bus = np.array([0])\n    else:\n        ref_bus = ref_bus[0]\n\n    # ===== Create Optimization Problem =====\n    opti = ca.Opti()\n\n    # Variables: voltage magnitudes and angles\n    vm = opti.variable(n_bus)  # voltage magnitudes (p.u.)\n    va = opti.variable(n_bus)  # voltage angles (radians)\n\n    # Generator dispatch\n    pg = opti.variable(n_gen)  # real power\n    qg = opti.variable(n_gen)  # reactive power\n\n    # ===== Objective: Minimize generation cost =====\n    cost = ca.sum1(c2 * pg**2 + c1 * pg + c0)\n    opti.minimize(cost)\n\n    # ===== Constraints =====\n\n    # Voltage magnitude bounds\n    opti.subject_to(vm >= vm_min)\n    opti.subject_to(vm <= vm_max)\n\n    # Generator bounds (only active generators)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[i] == 1:\n            opti.subject_to(pg[i] >= gen_pmin[i] / baseMVA)\n            opti.subject_to(pg[i] <= gen_pmax[i] / baseMVA)\n            opti.subject_to(qg[i] >= gen_qmin[i] / baseMVA)\n            opti.subject_to(qg[i] <= gen_qmax[i] / baseMVA)\n        else:\n            opti.subject_to(pg[i] == 0)\n            opti.subject_to(qg[i] == 0)\n\n    # Reference bus angle\n    opti.subject_to(va[ref_bus] == 0)\n\n    # Power flow constraints (nodal balance)\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        # Bus voltage as complex number\n        V_i = vm[i] * ca.exp(1j * va[i])\n\n        # Generation and load\n        p_gen = 0\n        q_gen = 0\n        for g in range(n_gen):\n            if gen_bus[g] == i:\n                p_gen += pg[g]\n                q_gen += qg[g]\n\n        # Convert load from MW/MVAr to p.u.\n        p_load = pd_mw[i] / baseMVA\n        q_load = qd_mvar[i] / baseMVA\n\n        # Power balance: S_gen - S_load = sum of branch flows\n        p_mismatch = 0\n        q_mismatch = 0\n\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            if Y[i, j] != 0:\n                V_j = vm[j] * ca.exp(1j * va[j])\n                S_ij = ca.conj(V_i) * (Y[i, j] * V_j)\n                p_mismatch += ca.real(S_ij)\n                q_mismatch += ca.imag(S_ij)\n\n        opti.subject_to(p_gen - p_load == p_mismatch)\n        opti.subject_to(q_gen - q_load == q_mismatch)\n\n    # Branch flow limits (apparent power)\n    for idx in range(n_branch):\n        if branch_status[idx] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        i, j = fbus[idx], tbus[idx]\n        t = T[idx]\n        y = y_series[idx]\n        limit = branch_limit[idx] / baseMVA\n\n        V_i = vm[i] * ca.exp(1j * va[i])\n        V_j = vm[j] * ca.exp(1j * va[j])\n\n        # Power flow from i to j\n        S_ij = (y / (ca.conj(t) * t) + 1j * np.imag(Y[i, i])) * ca.conj(V_i) * V_i - \\\n               (y / ca.conj(t)) * ca.conj(V_i) * V_j\n\n        # Power flow from j to i\n        S_ji = (y + 1j * np.imag(Y[j, j])) * ca.conj(V_j) * V_j - \\\n               (y / t) * ca.conj(V_j) * V_i\n\n        # Apparent power limits\n        opti.subject_to(ca.sqrt(ca.real(S_ij)**2 + ca.imag(S_ij)**2) <= limit)\n        opti.subject_to(ca.sqrt(ca.real(S_ji)**2 + ca.imag(S_ji)**2) <= limit)\n\n    # ===== Initial Guess =====\n    opti.set_initial(vm, 1.0)\n    opti.set_initial(va, 0.0)\n    opti.set_initial(pg, gen_pg / baseMVA)\n    opti.set_initial(qg, gen_qg / baseMVA)\n\n    # ===== Solver Setup =====\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt': {\n            'print_level': 0,\n            'max_iter': 3000,\n            'tol': 1e-6,\n            'acceptable_tol': 1e-4\n        }\n    }\n    opti.solver('ipopt', opts)\n\n    # ===== Solve =====\n    try:\n        sol = opti.solve()\n        status = 'optimal' if sol.stats()['return_status'] == 'Solve_Succeeded' else 'suboptimal'\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Solver failed: {e}\", file=sys.stderr)\n        sol = opti.debug\n        status = 'failed'\n\n    return sol, opti, status, (vm, va, pg, qg), (buses, gens, branches, Y, baseMVA)\n\ndef generate_report(sol, opti, status, variables, network_info) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Generate report.json from solver solution\"\"\"\n    vm, va, pg, qg = variables\n    buses, gens, branches, Y, baseMVA = network_info\n\n    # Extract solution values\n    vm_val = np.array(sol.value(vm))\n    va_val = np.array(sol.value(va)) * 180 / np.pi  # convert to degrees\n    pg_val = np.array(sol.value(pg)) * baseMVA  # convert to MW\n    qg_val = np.array(sol.value(qg)) * baseMVA  # convert to MVAr\n    cost_val = float(sol.value(opti.f))\n\n    n_gen = len(gens)\n    n_bus = len(buses)\n    n_branch = len(branches)\n\n    gen_bus = gens[:, 0].astype(int) - 1\n    gen_status = gens[:, 7]\n    gen_qmax = gens[:, 3]\n    gen_qmin = gens[:, 4]\n    gen_pmax = gens[:, 8]\n    gen_pmin = gens[:, 9]\n\n    bus_i = buses[:, 0].astype(int)\n    bus_vm_max = buses[:, 11]\n    bus_vm_min = buses[:, 12]\n\n    pd_mw = buses[:, 2]\n    qd_mvar = buses[:, 3]\n    total_load_mw = np.sum(pd_mw)\n    total_load_mvar = np.sum(qd_mvar)\n\n    # Calculate total generation and losses\n    total_gen_mw = np.sum(pg_val[gen_status == 1])\n    total_gen_mvar = np.sum(qg_val[gen_status == 1])\n    total_losses_mw = total_gen_mw - total_load_mw\n\n    # Build generators report\n    generators = []\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[i] == 1:\n            generators.append({\n                \"id\": int(i + 1),\n                \"bus\": int(gens[i, 0]),\n                \"pg_MW\": float(pg_val[i]),\n                \"qg_MVAr\": float(qg_val[i]),\n                \"pmin_MW\": float(gen_pmin[i]),\n                \"pmax_MW\": float(gen_pmax[i]),\n                \"qmin_MVAr\": float(gen_qmin[i]),\n                \"qmax_MVAr\": float(gen_qmax[i])\n            })\n\n    # Build buses report\n    buses_report = []\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        buses_report.append({\n            \"id\": int(bus_i[i]),\n            \"vm_pu\": float(vm_val[i]),\n            \"va_deg\": float(va_val[i]),\n            \"vmin_pu\": float(bus_vm_min[i]),\n            \"vmax_pu\": float(bus_vm_max[i])\n        })\n\n    # Calculate branch flows and find most loaded\n    branch_status = branches[:, 10]\n    fbus = branches[:, 0].astype(int) - 1\n    tbus = branches[:, 1].astype(int) - 1\n    limit_mva = branches[:, 5]  # rateA\n\n    T_vals = (np.where(branches[:, 8] > 0, branches[:, 8], 1.0) *\n              np.exp(1j * branches[:, 9] * np.pi / 180))\n    y_vals = 1.0 / (branches[:, 2] + 1j * branches[:, 3])\n    b_vals = branches[:, 4]\n\n    branch_data = []\n    for idx in range(n_branch):\n        if branch_status[idx] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        i, j = fbus[idx], tbus[idx]\n        V_i = vm_val[i] * np.exp(1j * va_val[i] * np.pi / 180)\n        V_j = vm_val[j] * np.exp(1j * va_val[j] * np.pi / 180)\n\n        t = T_vals[idx]\n        y = y_vals[idx]\n        b = b_vals[idx]\n\n        # Shunt admittance\n        y_shunt = 1j * b / 2\n\n        # Power flow from i to j\n        S_ij = (y / (np.conj(t) * t) + y_shunt) * np.conj(V_i) * V_i - \\\n               (y / np.conj(t)) * np.conj(V_i) * V_j\n\n        # Power flow from j to i\n        S_ji = (y + y_shunt) * np.conj(V_j) * V_j - \\\n               (y / t) * np.conj(V_j) * V_i\n\n        flow_from_mva = np.abs(S_ij) * baseMVA\n        flow_to_mva = np.abs(S_ji) * baseMVA\n\n        loading_pct = max(flow_from_mva, flow_to_mva) / limit_mva[idx] * 100\n\n        branch_data.append({\n            \"from_bus\": int(branches[idx, 0]),\n            \"to_bus\": int(branches[idx, 1]),\n            \"loading_pct\": float(loading_pct),\n            \"flow_from_MVA\": float(flow_from_mva),\n            \"flow_to_MVA\": float(flow_to_mva),\n            \"limit_MVA\": float(limit_mva[idx])\n        })\n\n    # Sort by loading and get top 10\n    branch_data.sort(key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n    most_loaded = branch_data[:10]\n\n    # Feasibility check\n    max_p_mismatch = 0\n    max_q_mismatch = 0\n    max_voltage_violation = 0\n    max_branch_overload = 0\n\n    # Check power balance\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        V_i = vm_val[i] * np.exp(1j * va_val[i] * np.pi / 180)\n        p_gen, q_gen = 0, 0\n        for g in range(n_gen):\n            if gen_bus[g] == i:\n                p_gen += pg_val[g]\n                q_gen += qg_val[g]\n\n        p_flow, q_flow = 0, 0\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            if Y[i, j] != 0:\n                V_j = vm_val[j] * np.exp(1j * va_val[j] * np.pi / 180)\n                S_ij = np.conj(V_i) * (Y[i, j] * V_j)\n                p_flow += np.real(S_ij) * baseMVA\n                q_flow += np.imag(S_ij) * baseMVA\n\n        p_mismatch = abs(p_gen - pd_mw[i] - p_flow)\n        q_mismatch = abs(q_gen - qd_mvar[i] - q_flow)\n        max_p_mismatch = max(max_p_mismatch, p_mismatch)\n        max_q_mismatch = max(max_q_mismatch, q_mismatch)\n\n    # Check voltage limits\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        violation = max(0, bus_vm_min[i] - vm_val[i]) + max(0, vm_val[i] - bus_vm_max[i])\n        max_voltage_violation = max(max_voltage_violation, violation)\n\n    # Check branch limits\n    for branch in branch_data:\n        overload = max(0, branch['flow_from_MVA'] - branch['limit_MVA'],\n                      branch['flow_to_MVA'] - branch['limit_MVA'])\n        max_branch_overload = max(max_branch_overload, overload)\n\n    # Build report\n    report = {\n        \"summary\": {\n            \"total_cost_per_hour\": float(cost_val),\n            \"total_load_MW\": float(total_load_mw),\n            \"total_load_MVAr\": float(total_load_mvar),\n            \"total_generation_MW\": float(total_gen_mw),\n            \"total_generation_MVAr\": float(total_gen_mvar),\n            \"total_losses_MW\": float(total_losses_mw),\n            \"solver_status\": status\n        },\n        \"generators\": generators,\n        \"buses\": buses_report,\n        \"most_loaded_branches\": most_loaded,\n        \"feasibility_check\": {\n            \"max_p_mismatch_MW\": float(max_p_mismatch),\n            \"max_q_mismatch_MVAr\": float(max_q_mismatch),\n            \"max_voltage_violation_pu\": float(max_voltage_violation),\n            \"max_branch_overload_MVA\": float(max_branch_overload)\n        }\n    }\n\n    return report\n\ndef main():\n    # Load network\n    print(\"Loading network data...\", file=sys.stderr)\n    network = load_network('/root/network.json')\n\n    # Solve AC OPF\n    print(\"Solving AC OPF...\", file=sys.stderr)\n    sol, opti, status, variables, network_info = solve_ac_opf(network)\n\n    # Generate report\n    print(\"Generating report...\", file=sys.stderr)\n    report = generate_report(sol, opti, status, variables, network_info)\n\n    # Write report\n    with open('/root/report.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(report, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(f\"Report generated: solver_status = {report['summary']['solver_status']}\",\n          file=sys.stderr)\n    print(f\"Total cost: ${report['summary']['total_cost_per_hour']:,.2f}/hour\")\n    print(f\"Total
