MATPOWER branch format: [fbus tbus r x b rateA rateB rateC ratio angle status angmin angmax]\n    fbus = branches[:, 0].astype(int) - 1  # Convert to 0-indexing\n    tbus = branches[:, 1].astype(int) - 1\n    r = branches[:, 2]\n    x = branches[:, 3]\n    b = branches[:, 4]\n    ratio = branches[:, 8]  # transformer ratio\n    angle_shift = branches[:, 9] * np.pi / 180  # phase shift in radians\n    status = branches[:, 10]\n\n    # Series impedance and admittance\n    z = r + 1j * x\n    y_series = np.where(z != 0, 1.0 / z, 0)\n\n    # Transformer ratios (default 1.0 for lines)\n    T = np.where(ratio > 0, ratio, 1.0) * np.exp(1j * angle_shift)\n\n    # Shunt admittance at each bus\n    y_shunt_i = 1j * b / 2\n    y_shunt_j = 1j * b / 2\n\n    # Build full admittance matrix\n    Y = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n\n    for idx in range(len(branches)):\n        if status[idx] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        i, j = fbus[idx], tbus[idx]\n        t = T[idx]\n        y = y_series[idx]\n\n        # Off-diagonal elements\n        Y[i, j] -= y / np.conj(t)\n        Y[j, i] -= y / t\n\n        # Diagonal elements\n        Y[i, i] += y / (np.conj(t) * t) + y_shunt_i[idx]\n        Y[j, j] += y + y_shunt_j[idx]\n\n    # Add bus shunts\n    buses = np.array(network['bus'])\n    gs = buses[:, 4]\n    bs = buses[:, 5]\n    Y += np.diag(gs + 1j * bs)\n\n    return Y, y_series, T, y_shunt_i, y_shunt_j, status\n\ndef solve_ac_opf(network: Dict) -> Tuple[Dict, str]:\n    \"\"\"Solve AC OPF using CasADi and IPOPT\n\n    Returns:\n        sol: solver solution with all variables\n        status: solver status string\n    \"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    buses = np.array(network['bus'])\n    gens = np.array(network['gen'])\n    gencost = np.array(network['gencost'])\n    branches = np.array(network['branch'])\n\n    n_bus = len(buses)\n    n_gen = len(gens)\n    n_branch = len(branches)\n\n    # MATPOWER indices (convert to 0-based)\n    bus_type = buses[:, 1].astype(int)  # 1=PQ, 2=PV, 3=ref\n    pd_mw = buses[:, 2]  # Load P\n    qd_mvar = buses[:, 3]  # Load Q\n    vm_max = buses[:, 11]\n    vm_min = buses[:, 12]\n\n    gen_bus = gens[:, 0].astype(int) - 1\n    gen_pg = gens[:, 1]  # Initial P\n    gen_qg = gens[:, 2]  # Initial Q\n    gen_qmax = gens[:, 3]\n    gen_qmin = gens[:, 4]\n    gen_status = gens[:, 7]\n    gen_pmax = gens[:, 8]\n    gen_pmin = gens[:, 9]\n\n    # Cost function: c2*P^2 + c1*P + c0\n    cost_type = gencost[:, 0].astype(int)\n    cost_n = gencost[:, 3].astype(int)\n    c2 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    c1 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    c0 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if cost_type[i] == 2:  # Polynomial cost\n            n = cost_n[i]\n            if n >= 3:\n                c2[i] = gencost[i, 4]\n                c1[i] = gencost[i, 5] if n >= 3 else 0\n                c0[i] = gencost[i, 6] if n >= 3 else 0\n\n    # Build admittance matrix\n    Y, y_series, T, y_shunt_i, y_shunt_j, branch_status = build_admittance_matrix(network)\n\n    # Branch data\n    fbus = branches[:, 0].astype(int) - 1\n    tbus = branches[:, 1].astype(int) - 1\n    branch_limit = branches[:, 5]  # rateA in MVA\n\n    # Reference bus (slack bus)\n    ref_bus = np.where(bus_type == 3)[0]\n    if len(ref_bus) == 0:\n        ref_bus = 0\n    else:\n        ref_bus = ref_bus[0]\n\n    # ===== Decision Variables =====\n    Vm = ca.MX.sym(\"Vm\", n_bus)  # voltage magnitudes (p.u.)\n    Va = ca.MX.sym(\"Va\", n_bus)  # voltage angles (radians)\n    Pg = ca.MX.sym(\"Pg\", n_gen)  # real power (p.u.)\n    Qg = ca.MX.sym(\"Qg\", n_gen)  # reactive power (p.u.)\n\n    x = ca.vertcat(Vm, Va, Pg, Qg)\n\n    # ===== Objective: Minimize generation cost =====\n    cost = ca.MX(0)\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        Pg_mw = Pg[k] * baseMVA\n        cost += c2[k] * Pg_mw**2 + c1[k] * Pg_mw + c0[k]\n\n    # ===== Constraints =====\n    g_expr = []\n    lbg = []\n    ubg = []\n\n    # Power flow equations (nodal balance)\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        # Bus voltage as complex number\n        V_i = Vm[i] * ca.cos(Va[i]) + 1j * Vm[i] * ca.sin(Va[i])\n\n        # Generation and load\n        p_gen = ca.MX(0)\n        q_gen = ca.MX(0)\n        for g in range(n_gen):\n            if gen_bus[g] == i:\n                p_gen += Pg[g]\n                q_gen += Qg[g]\n\n        # Convert load from MW/MVAr to p.u.\n        p_load = pd_mw[i] / baseMVA\n        q_load = qd_mvar[i] / baseMVA\n\n        # Power balance: sum of branch flows = injection - load\n        p_flow = ca.MX(0)\n        q_flow = ca.MX(0)\n\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            if Y[i, j] != 0 or i == j:\n                V_j = Vm[j] * ca.cos(Va[j]) + 1j * Vm[j] * ca.sin(Va[j])\n\n                # Current from i to j: I_ij = (V_i - V_j) * Y_ij\n                # Power: S_ij = V_i * conj(I_ij)\n                Y_ij = Y[i, j]\n                S_ij = V_i * ca.conj(Y_ij * V_j)\n\n                p_flow += ca.real(S_ij)\n                q_flow += ca.imag(S_ij)\n\n        # Power balance equations\n        g_expr.append(p_gen - p_load - p_flow)\n        lbg.append(0.0)\n        ubg.append(0.0)\n\n        g_expr.append(q_gen - q_load - q_flow)\n        lbg.append(0.0)\n        ubg.append(0.0)\n\n    # Reference bus angle = 0\n    g_expr.append(Va[ref_bus])\n    lbg.append(0.0)\n    ubg.append(0.0)\n\n    g = ca.vertcat(*g_expr)\n\n    # ===== Variable Bounds =====\n    lbx = []\n    ubx = []\n\n    # Voltage magnitude bounds\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        lbx.append(vm_min[i])\n        ubx.append(vm_max[i])\n\n    # Voltage angle bounds (allow -pi to pi)\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        lbx.append(-np.pi)\n        ubx.append(np.pi)\n\n    # Generator real power bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[i] == 1:\n            lbx.append(gen_pmin[i] / baseMVA)\n            ubx.append(gen_pmax[i] / baseMVA)\n        else:\n            lbx.append(0.0)\n            ubx.append(0.0)\n\n    # Generator reactive power bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[i] == 1:\n            lbx.append(gen_qmin[i] / baseMVA)\n            ubx.append(gen_qmax[i] / baseMVA)\n        else:\n            lbx.append(0.0)\n            ubx.append(0.0)\n\n    # ===== Initial Guess =====\n    x0 = []\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        x0.append(1.0)  # Vm\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        x0.append(0.0)  # Va\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        x0.append(gen_pg[i] / baseMVA)  # Pg\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        x0.append(gen_qg[i] / baseMVA)  # Qg\n\n    # Enforce reference bus angle = 0\n    x0[n_bus + ref_bus] = 0.0\n\n    # ===== Solver Setup =====\n    nlp = {\"x\": x, \"f\": cost, \"g\": g}\n    opts = {\n        \"ipopt.print_level\": 0,\n        \"ipopt.max_iter\": 3000,\n        \"ipopt.tol\": 1e-6,\n        \"ipopt.acceptable_tol\": 1e-4,\n        \"ipopt.mu_strategy\": \"adaptive\",\n        \"print_time\": False,\n    }\n\n    try:\n        solver = ca.nlpsol(\"solver\", \"ipopt\", nlp, opts)\n        sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg, ubg=ubg)\n\n        # Check solver status\n        stats = solver.stats()\n        if stats['return_status'] == 'Solve_Succeeded':\n            status = 'optimal'\n        else:\n            status = 'suboptimal'\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Solver error: {e}\", file=sys.stderr)\n        status = 'failed'\n        sol = None\n\n    return sol, status, (Vm, Va, Pg, Qg), (buses, gens, branches, Y, baseMVA,\n                                            fbus, tbus, branch_status, branch_limit,\n                                            gen_bus, gen_status, pd_mw, qd_mvar)\n\ndef generate_report(sol, status, variables, network_info) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Generate report.json from solver solution\"\"\"\n    if sol is None:\n        return {\"error\": \"Solver failed\"}\n\n    Vm, Va, Pg, Qg = variables\n    (buses, gens, branches, Y, baseMVA,\n     fbus, tbus, branch_status, branch_limit,\n     gen_bus, gen_status, pd_mw, qd_mvar) = network_info\n\n    # Extract solution values\n    x_opt = np.array(sol[\"x\"]).flatten()\n\n    n_bus = len(buses)\n    n_gen = len(gens)\n    n_branch = len(branches)\n\n    vm_val = x_opt[:n_bus]\n    va_val = x_opt[n_bus:2*n_bus] * 180 / np.pi  # convert to degrees\n    pg_val = x_opt[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen] * baseMVA  # convert to MW\n    qg_val = x_opt[2*n_bus+n_gen:] * baseMVA  # convert to MVAr\n    cost_val = float(sol[\"f\"])\n\n    gen_qmax = gens[:, 3]\n    gen_qmin = gens[:, 4]\n    gen_pmax = gens[:, 8]\n    gen_pmin = gens[:, 9]\n\n    bus_i = buses[:, 0].astype(int)\n    bus_vm_max = buses[:, 11]\n    bus_vm_min = buses[:, 12]\n\n    total_load_mw = np.sum(pd_mw)\n    total_load_mvar = np.sum(qd_mvar)\n\n    # Calculate total generation and losses\n    total_gen_mw = np.sum(pg_val[gen_status == 1])\n    total_gen_mvar = np.sum(qg_val[gen_status == 1])\n    total_losses_mw = total_gen_mw - total_load_mw\n\n    # Build generators report\n    generators = []\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[i] == 1:\n            generators.append({\n                \"id\": int(i + 1),\n                \"bus\": int(gens[i, 0]),\n                \"pg_MW\": float(pg_val[i]),\n                \"qg_MVAr\": float(qg_val[i]),\n                \"pmin_MW\": float(gen_pmin[i]),\n                \"pmax_MW\": float(gen_pmax[i]),\n                \"qmin_MVAr\": float(gen_qmin[i]),\n                \"qmax_MVAr\": float(gen_qmax[i])\n            })\n\n    # Build buses report\n    buses_report = []\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        buses_report.append({\n            \"id\": int(bus_i[i]),\n            \"vm_pu\": float(vm_val[i]),\n            \"va_deg\": float(va_val[i]),\n            \"vmin_pu\": float(bus_vm_min[i]),\n            \"vmax_pu\": float(bus_vm_max[i])\n        })\n\n    # Calculate branch flows and find most loaded\n    branch_data = []\n    for idx in range(n_branch):\n        if branch_status[idx] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        i, j = fbus[idx], tbus[idx]\n        V_i = vm_val[i] * np.exp(1j * va_val[i] * np.pi / 180)\n        V_j = vm_val[j] * np.exp(1j * va_val[j] * np.pi / 180)\n\n        Y_ij = Y[i, j]\n        Y_ji = Y[j, i]\n\n        # Power flow from i to j\n        S_ij = V_i * np.conj(Y_ij * V_j)\n        flow_from_mva = np.abs(S_ij) * baseMVA\n\n        # Power flow from j to i\n        S_ji = V_j * np.conj(Y_ji * V_i)\n        flow_to_mva = np.abs(S_ji) * baseMVA\n\n        limit = branch_limit[idx]\n        loading_pct = max(flow_from_mva, flow_to_mva) / limit * 100\n\n        branch_data.append({\n            \"from_bus\": int(branches[idx, 0]),\n            \"to_bus\": int(branches[idx, 1]),\n            \"loading_pct\": float(loading_pct),\n            \"flow_from_MVA\": float(flow_from_mva),\n            \"flow_to_MVA\": float(flow_to_mva),\n            \"limit_MVA\": float(limit)\n        })\n\n    # Sort by loading and get top 10\n    branch_data.sort(key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n    most_loaded = branch_data[:10]\n\n    # Feasibility check\n    max_p_mismatch = 0\n    max_q_mismatch = 0\n    max_voltage_violation = 0\n    max_branch_overload = 0\n\n    # Check power balance\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        V_i = vm_val[i] * np.exp(1j * va_val[i] * np.pi / 180)\n        p_gen, q_gen = 0, 0\n        for g in range(n_gen):\n            if gen_bus[g] == i:\n                p_gen += pg_val[g]\n                q_gen += qg_val[g]\n\n        p_flow, q_flow = 0, 0\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            if Y[i, j] != 0:\n                V_j = vm_val[j] * np.exp(1j * va_val[j] * np.pi / 180)\n                S_ij = V_i * np.conj(Y[i, j] * V_j)\n                p_flow += np.real(S_ij) * baseMVA\n                q_flow += np.imag(S_ij) * baseMVA\n\n        p_mismatch = abs(p_gen - pd_mw[i] - p_flow)\n        q_mismatch = abs(q_gen - qd_mvar[i] - q_flow)\n        max_p_mismatch = max(max_p_mismatch, p_mismatch)\n        max_q_mismatch = max(max_q_mismatch, q_mismatch)\n\n    # Check voltage limits\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        violation = max(0, bus_vm_min[i] - vm_val[i]) + max(0, vm_val[i] - bus_vm_max[i])\n        max_voltage_violation = max(max_voltage_violation, violation)\n\n    # Check branch limits\n    for branch in branch_data:\n        overload = max(0, branch['flow_from_MVA'] - branch['limit_MVA'],\n                      branch['flow_to_MVA'] - branch['limit_MVA'])\n        max_branch_overload = max(max_branch_overload, overload)\n\n    # Build report\n    report = {\n        \"summary\": {\n            \"total_cost_per_hour\": float(cost_val),\n            \"total_load_MW\": float(total_load_mw),\n            \"total_load_MVAr\": float(total_load_mvar),\n            \"total_generation_MW\": float(total_gen_mw),\n            \"total_generation_MVAr\": float(total_gen_mvar),\n            \"total_losses_MW\": float(total_losses_mw),\n            \"solver_status\": status\n        },\n        \"generators\": generators,\n        \"buses\": buses_report,\n        \"most_loaded_branches\": most_loaded,\n        \"feasibility_check\": {\n            \"max_p_mismatch_MW\": float(max_p_mismatch),\n            \"max_q_mismatch_MVAr\": float(max_q_mismatch),\n            \"max_voltage_violation_pu\": float(max_voltage_violation),\n            \"max_branch_overload_MVA\": float(max_branch_overload)\n        }\n    }\n\n    return report\n\ndef main():\n    # V_i * conj(Y_ij * V_j) = (V_i_r + j*V_i_i) * (Yv_j_r - j*Yv_j_i)","+                # = V_i_r*Yv_j_r + V_i_i*Yv_j_i + j*(V_i_i*Yv_j_r - V_i_r*Yv_j_i)","+                S_ij_real = Vm_i_real * Yv_j_real + Vm_i_imag * Yv_j_imag","+                S_ij_imag = Vm_i_imag * Yv_j_real - Vm_i_real * Yv_j_imag","+","+                p_flow += S_ij_real","+                q_flow += S_ij_imag","+","         # Power balance equations","         g_expr.append(p_gen - p_load - p_flow)","         lbg.append(0.0)"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

Solution exists but suboptimal\n            status = 'suboptimal'\n            best_sol = sol\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Solver error: {e}\", file=sys.stderr)\n        status = 'failed'\n        best_sol = None\n\n    return best_sol, status, (Vm, Va, Pg, Qg), (buses, gens, branches, Y, baseMVA,\n                                                 fbus, tbus, branch_status, branch_limit,\n                                                 gen_bus, gen_status, pd_mw, qd_mvar)","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nAC Optimal Power Flow Solver for ISO Base Case Planning\nUses CasADi and IPOPT to find least-cost AC-feasible operating point\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nimport casadi as ca\nfrom typing import Dict, Tuple\nimport sys\n\ndef load_network(filepath: str) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Load MATPOWER format network data\"\"\"\n    with open(filepath, 'r') as f:\n        return json.load(f)\n\ndef build_admittance_matrix(network: Dict) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray, np.ndarray,\n                                                     np.ndarray, np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:\n    \"\"\"Build admittance matrix from branch data\n\n    Returns:\n        Y: complex admittance matrix\n        y_ser: series admittances for each branch\n        T_ratio: transformer ratios for each branch\n        y_shunt_i, y_shunt_j: shunt admittances at each end\n        branch_status: status of each branch\n    \"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    branches = np.array(network['branch'])\n    n_bus = len(network['bus'])\n\n    # MATPOWER branch format: [fbus tbus r x b rateA rateB rateC ratio angle status angmin angmax]\n    fbus = branches[:, 0].astype(int) - 1  # Convert to 0-indexing\n    tbus = branches[:, 1].astype(int) - 1\n    r = branches[:, 2]\n    x = branches[:, 3]\n    b = branches[:, 4]\n    ratio = branches[:, 8]  # transformer ratio\n    angle_shift = branches[:, 9] * np.pi / 180  # phase shift in radians\n    status = branches[:, 10]\n\n    # Series impedance and admittance\n    z = r + 1j * x\n    y_series = np.where(z != 0, 1.0 / z, 0)\n\n    # Transformer ratios (default 1.0 for lines)\n    T = np.where(ratio > 0, ratio, 1.0) * np.exp(1j * angle_shift)\n\n    # Shunt admittance at each bus\n    y_shunt_i = 1j * b / 2\n    y_shunt_j = 1j * b / 2\n\n    # Build full admittance matrix\n    Y = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n\n    for idx in range(len(branches)):\n        if status[idx] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        i, j = fbus[idx], tbus[idx]\n        t = T[idx]\n        y = y_series[idx]\n\n        # Off-diagonal elements\n        Y[i, j] -= y / np.conj(t)\n        Y[j, i] -= y / t\n\n        # Diagonal elements\n        Y[i, i] += y / (np.conj(t) * t) + y_shunt_i[idx]\n        Y[j, j] += y + y_shunt_j[idx]\n\n    # Add bus shunts\n    buses = np.array(network['bus'])\n    gs = buses[:, 4]\n    bs = buses[:, 5]\n    Y += np.diag(gs + 1j * bs)\n\n    return Y, y_series, T, y_shunt_i, y_shunt_j, status\n\ndef solve_ac_opf(network: Dict) -> Tuple[Dict, str]:\n    \"\"\"Solve AC OPF using CasADi and IPOPT\n\n    Returns:\n        sol: solver solution with all variables\n        status: solver status string\n    \"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    buses = np.array(network['bus'])\n    gens = np.array(network['gen'])\n    gencost = np.array(network['gencost'])\n    branches = np.array(network['branch'])\n\n    n_bus = len(buses)\n    n_gen = len(gens)\n    n_branch = len(branches)\n\n    # MATPOWER indices (convert to 0-based)\n    bus_type = buses[:, 1].astype(int)  # 1=PQ, 2=PV, 3=ref\n    pd_mw = buses[:, 2]  # Load P\n    qd_mvar = buses[:, 3]  # Load Q\n    vm_max = buses[:, 11]\n    vm_min = buses[:, 12]\n\n    gen_bus = gens[:, 0].astype(int) - 1\n    gen_pg = gens[:, 1]  # Initial P\n    gen_qg = gens[:, 2]  # Initial Q\n    gen_qmax = gens[:, 3]\n    gen_qmin = gens[:, 4]\n    gen_status = gens[:, 7]\n    gen_pmax = gens[:, 8]\n    gen_pmin = gens[:, 9]\n\n    # Cost function: c2*P^2 + c1*P + c0\n    cost_type = gencost[:, 0].astype(int)\n    cost_n = gencost[:, 3].astype(int)\n    c2 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    c1 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    c0 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if cost_type[i] == 2:  # Polynomial cost\n            n = cost_n[i]\n            if n >= 3:\n                c2[i] = gencost[i, 4]\n                c1[i] = gencost[i, 5] if n >= 3 else 0\n                c0[i] = gencost[i, 6] if n >= 3 else 0\n\n    # Build admittance matrix\n    Y, y_series, T, y_shunt_i, y_shunt_j, branch_status = build_admittance_matrix(network)\n\n    # Branch data\n    fbus = branches[:, 0].astype(int) - 1\n    tbus = branches[:, 1].astype(int) - 1\n    branch_limit = branches[:, 5]  # rateA in MVA\n\n    # Reference bus (slack bus)\n    ref_bus = np.where(bus_type == 3)[0]\n    if len(ref_bus) == 0:\n        ref_bus = 0\n    else:\n        ref_bus = ref_bus[0]\n\n    # ===== Decision Variables =====\n    Vm = ca.MX.sym(\"Vm\", n_bus)  # voltage magnitudes (p.u.)\n    Va = ca.MX.sym(\"Va\", n_bus)  # voltage angles (radians)\n    Pg = ca.MX.sym(\"Pg\", n_gen)  # real power (p.u.)\n    Qg = ca.MX.sym(\"Qg\", n_gen)  # reactive power (p.u.)\n\n    x = ca.vertcat(Vm, Va, Pg, Qg)\n\n    # ===== Objective: Minimize generation cost =====\n    cost = ca.MX(0)\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        Pg_mw = Pg[k] * baseMVA\n        cost += c2[k] * Pg_mw**2 + c1[k] * Pg_mw + c0[k]\n\n    # ===== Constraints =====\n    g_expr = []\n    lbg = []\n    ubg = []\n\n    # Power flow equations (nodal balance)\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        # Bus voltage as real and imaginary parts\n        Vm_i = Vm[i]\n        Va_i = Va[i]\n        Vm_i_real = Vm_i * ca.cos(Va_i)\n        Vm_i_imag = Vm_i * ca.sin(Va_i)\n\n        # Generation and load\n        p_gen = ca.MX(0)\n        q_gen = ca.MX(0)\n        for g in range(n_gen):\n            if gen_bus[g] == i:\n                p_gen += Pg[g]\n                q_gen += Qg[g]\n\n        # Convert load from MW/MVAr to p.u.\n        p_load = pd_mw[i] / baseMVA\n        q_load = qd_mvar[i] / baseMVA\n\n        # Power balance: sum of branch flows = injection - load\n        p_flow = ca.MX(0)\n        q_flow = ca.MX(0)\n\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            if Y[i, j] != 0 or i == j:\n                Vm_j = Vm[j]\n                Va_j = Va[j]\n                Vm_j_real = Vm_j * ca.cos(Va_j)\n                Vm_j_imag = Vm_j * ca.sin(Va_j)\n\n                # Y_ij components\n                Y_ij = Y[i, j]\n                Y_ij_real = np.real(Y_ij)\n                Y_ij_imag = np.imag(Y_ij)\n\n                # V_i * conj(Y_ij * V_j)\n                # = (V_i_r + j*V_i_i) * ((Y_r - j*Y_i) * (V_j_r + j*V_j_i))\n                # Y_ij * V_j = (Y_r*V_j_r - Y_i*V_j_i) + j*(Y_r*V_j_i + Y_i*V_j_r)\n                Yv_j_real = Y_ij_real * Vm_j_real - Y_ij_imag * Vm_j_imag\n                Yv_j_imag = Y_ij_real * Vm_j_imag + Y_ij_imag * Vm_j_real\n\n                # V_i * conj(Y_ij * V_j) = (V_i_r + j*V_i_i) * (Yv_j_r - j*Yv_j_i)\n                # = V_i_r*Yv_j_r + V_i_i*Yv_j_i + j*(V_i_i*Yv_j_r - V_i_r*Yv_j_i)\n                S_ij_real = Vm_i_real * Yv_j_real + Vm_i_imag * Yv_j_imag\n                S_ij_imag = Vm_i_imag * Yv_j_real - Vm_i_real * Yv_j_imag\n\n                p_flow += S_ij_real\n                q_flow += S_ij_imag\n\n        # Power balance equations\n        g_expr.append(p_gen - p_load - p_flow)\n        lbg.append(0.0)\n        ubg.append(0.0)\n\n        g_expr.append(q_gen - q_load - q_flow)\n        lbg.append(0.0)\n        ubg.append(0.0)\n\n    # Reference bus angle = 0\n    g_expr.append(Va[ref_bus])\n    lbg.append(0.0)\n    ubg.append(0.0)\n\n    g = ca.vertcat(*g_expr)\n\n    # ===== Variable Bounds =====\n    lbx = []\n    ubx = []\n\n    # Voltage magnitude bounds\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        lbx.append(vm_min[i])\n        ubx.append(vm_max[i])\n\n    # Voltage angle bounds (allow -pi to pi)\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        lbx.append(-np.pi)\n        ubx.append(np.pi)\n\n    # Generator real power bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[i] == 1:\n            lbx.append(gen_pmin[i] / baseMVA)\n            ubx.append(gen_pmax[i] / baseMVA)\n        else:\n            lbx.append(0.0)\n            ubx.append(0.0)\n\n    # Generator reactive power bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[i] == 1:\n            lbx.append(gen_qmin[i] / baseMVA)\n            ubx.append(gen_qmax[i] / baseMVA)\n        else:\n            lbx.append(0.0)\n            ubx.append(0.0)\n\n    # ===== Initial Guess =====\n    x0 = []\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        x0.append(1.0)  # Vm\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        x0.append(0.0)  # Va\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        x0.append(gen_pg[i] / baseMVA)  # Pg\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        x0.append(gen_qg[i] / baseMVA)  # Qg\n\n    # Enforce reference bus angle = 0\n    x0[n_bus + ref_bus] = 0.0\n\n    # ===== Solver Setup =====\n    nlp = {\"x\": x, \"f\": cost, \"g\": g}\n    opts = {\n        \"ipopt.print_level\": 0,\n        \"ipopt.max_iter\": 3000,\n        \"ipopt.tol\": 1e-6,\n        \"ipopt.acceptable_tol\": 1e-4,\n        \"ipopt.mu_strategy\": \"adaptive\",\n        \"print_time\": False,\n    }\n\n    try:\n        solver = ca.nlpsol(\"solver\", \"ipopt\", nlp, opts)\n        sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg, ubg=ubg)\n\n        # Check solver status\n        stats = solver.stats()\n        if stats['return_status'] == 'Solve_Succeeded':\n            status = 'optimal'\n        else:\n            status = 'suboptimal'\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Solver error: {e}\", file=sys.stderr)\n        status = 'failed'\n        sol = None\n\n    return sol, status, (Vm, Va, Pg, Qg), (buses, gens, branches, Y, baseMVA,\n                                            fbus, tbus, branch_status, branch_limit,\n                                            gen_bus, gen_status, pd_mw, qd_mvar)\n\ndef generate_report(sol, status, variables, network_info) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Generate report.json from solver solution\"\"\"\n    if sol is None:\n        return {\"error\": \"Solver failed\"}\n\n    Vm, Va, Pg, Qg = variables\n    (buses, gens, branches, Y, baseMVA,\n     fbus, tbus, branch_status, branch_limit,\n     gen_bus, gen_status, pd_mw, qd_mvar) = network_info\n\n    # Extract solution values\n    x_opt = np.array(sol[\"x\"]).flatten()\n\n    n_bus = len(buses)\n    n_gen = len(gens)\n    n_branch = len(branches)\n\n    vm_val = x_opt[:n_bus]\n    va_val = x_opt[n_bus:2*n_bus] * 180 / np.pi  # convert to degrees\n    pg_val = x_opt[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen] * baseMVA  # convert to MW\n    qg_val = x_opt[2*n_bus+n_gen:] * baseMVA  # convert to MVAr\n    cost_val = float(sol[\"f\"])\n\n    gen_qmax = gens[:, 3]\n    gen_qmin = gens[:, 4]\n    gen_pmax = gens[:, 8]\n    gen_pmin = gens[:, 9]\n\n    bus_i = buses[:, 0].astype(int)\n    bus_vm_max = buses[:, 11]\n    bus_vm_min = buses[:, 12]\n\n    total_load_mw = np.sum(pd_mw)\n    total_load_mvar = np.sum(qd_mvar)\n\n    # Calculate total generation and losses\n    total_gen_mw = np.sum(pg_val[gen_status == 1])\n    total_gen_mvar = np.sum(qg_val[gen_status == 1])\n    total_losses_mw = total_gen_mw - total_load_mw\n\n    # Build generators report\n    generators = []\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[i] == 1:\n            generators.append({\n                \"id\": int(i + 1),\n                \"bus\": int(gens[i, 0]),\n                \"pg_MW\": float(pg_val[i]),\n                \"qg_MVAr\": float(qg_val[i]),\n                \"pmin_MW\": float(gen_pmin[i]),\n                \"pmax_MW\": float(gen_pmax[i]),\n                \"qmin_MVAr\": float(gen_qmin[i]),\n                \"qmax_MVAr\": float(gen_qmax[i])\n            })\n\n    # Build buses report\n    buses_report = []\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        buses_report.append({\n            \"id\": int(bus_i[i]),\n            \"vm_pu\": float(vm_val[i]),\n            \"va_deg\": float(va_val[i]),\n            \"vmin_pu\": float(bus_vm_min[i]),\n            \"vmax_pu\": float(bus_vm_max[i])\n        })\n\n    # Calculate branch flows and find most loaded\n    branch_data = []\n    for idx in range(n_branch):\n        if branch_status[idx] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        i, j = fbus[idx], tbus[idx]\n        V_i = vm_val[i] * np.exp(1j * va_val[i] * np.pi / 180)\n        V_j = vm_val[j] * np.exp(1j * va_val[j] * np.pi / 180)\n\n        Y_ij = Y[i, j]\n        Y_ji = Y[j, i]\n\n        # Power flow from i to j\n        S_ij = V_i * np.conj(Y_ij * V_j)\n        flow_from_mva = np.abs(S_ij) * baseMVA\n\n        # Power flow from j to i\n        S_ji = V_j * np.conj(Y_ji * V_i)\n        flow_to_mva = np.abs(S_ji) * baseMVA\n\n        limit = branch_limit[idx]\n        loading_pct = max(flow_from_mva, flow_to_mva) / limit * 100\n\n        branch_data.append({\n            \"from_bus\": int(branches[idx, 0]),\n            \"to_bus\": int(branches[idx, 1]),\n            \"loading_pct\": float(loading_pct),\n            \"flow_from_MVA\": float(flow_from_mva),\n            \"flow_to_MVA\": float(flow_to_mva),\n            \"limit_MVA\": float(limit)\n        })\n\n    # Check power balance\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        V_i = vm_val[i] * np.exp(1j * va_val[i] * np.pi / 180)\n        p_gen, q_gen = 0, 0\n        for g in range(n_gen):\n            if gen_bus[g] == i:\n                p_gen += pg_val[g]\n                q_gen += qg_val[g]\n\n        p_flow, q_flow = 0, 0\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            if Y[i, j] != 0:\n                V_j = vm_val[j] * np.exp(1j * va_val[j] * np.pi / 180)\n                S_ij = V_i * np.conj(Y[i, j] * V_j)\n                p_flow += np.real(S_ij) * baseMVA\n                q_flow += np.imag(S_ij) * baseMVA\n\n        p_mismatch = abs(p_gen - pd_mw[i] - p_flow)\n        q_mismatch = abs(q_gen - qd_mvar[i] - q_flow)\n        max_p_mismatch = max(max_p_mismatch, p_mismatch)\n        max_q_mismatch = max(max_q_mismatch, q_mismatch)\n\n    # Check voltage limits\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        violation = max(0, bus_vm_min[i] - vm_val[i]) + max(0, vm_val[i] - bus_vm_max[i])\n        max_voltage_violation = max(max_voltage_violation, violation)\n\n    # Check branch limits\n    for branch in branch_data:\n        overload = max(0, branch['flow_from_MVA'] - branch['limit_MVA'],\n                      branch['flow_to_MVA'] - branch['limit_MVA'])\n        max_branch_overload = max(max_branch_overload, overload)\n\n    # Build report\n    report = {\n        \"summary\": {\n            \"total_cost_per_hour\": float(cost_val),\n            \"total_load_MW\": float(total_load_mw),\n            \"total_load_MVAr\": float(total_load_mvar),\n            \"total_generation_MW\": float(total_gen_mw),\n            \"total_generation_MVAr\": float(total_gen_mvar),\n            \"total_losses_MW\": float(total_losses_mw),\n            \"solver_status\": status\n        },\n        \"generators\": generators,\n        \"buses\": buses_report,\n        \"most_loaded_branches\": most_loaded,\n        \"feasibility_check\": {\n            \"max_p_mismatch_MW\": float(max_p_mismatch),\n            \"max_q_mismatch_MVAr\": float(max_q_mismatch),\n            \"max_voltage_violation_pu\": float(max_voltage_violation),\n            \"max_branch_overload_MVA\": float(max_branch_overload)\n        }\n    }\n\n    return report\n\ndef main():\n    # MATPOWER branch format: [fbus tbus r x b rateA rateB rateC ratio angle status angmin angmax]\n    fbus = branches[:, 0].astype(int) - 1  # Convert to 0-indexing\n    tbus = branches[:, 1].astype(int) - 1\n    r = branches[:, 2]\n    x = branches[:, 3]\n    b = branches[:, 4]\n    ratio = branches[:, 8]  # transformer ratio\n    angle_shift = branches[:, 9] * np.pi / 180  # phase shift in radians\n    status = branches[:, 10]\n\n    # Series impedance and admittance\n    z = r + 1j * x\n    y_series = np.where(z != 0, 1.0 / z, 0)\n\n    # Transformer ratios (default 1.0 for lines)\n    T = np.where(ratio > 0, ratio, 1.0) * np.exp(1j * angle_shift)\n\n    # Shunt admittance at each bus\n    y_shunt_i = 1j * b / 2\n    y_shunt_j = 1j * b / 2\n\n    # Build full admittance matrix\n    Y = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n\n    for idx in range(len(branches)):\n        if status[idx] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        i, j = fbus[idx], tbus[idx]\n        t = T[idx]\n        y = y_series[idx]\n\n        # Off-diagonal elements\n        Y[i, j] -= y / np.conj(t)\n        Y[j, i] -= y / t\n\n        # Diagonal elements\n        Y[i, i] += y / (np.conj(t) * t) + y_shunt_i[idx]\n        Y[j, j] += y + y_shunt_j[idx]\n\n    # Add bus shunts\n    buses = np.array(network['bus'])\n    gs = buses[:, 4]\n    bs = buses[:, 5]\n    Y += np.diag(gs + 1j * bs)\n\n    return Y, y_series, T, y_shunt_i, y_shunt_j, status\n\ndef solve_ac_opf(network: Dict) -> Tuple[Dict, str]:\n    \"\"\"Solve AC OPF using CasADi and IPOPT\n\n    Returns:\n        sol: solver solution with all variables\n        status: solver status string\n    \"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    buses = np.array(network['bus'])\n    gens = np.array(network['gen'])\n    gencost = np.array(network['gencost'])\n    branches = np.array(network['branch'])\n\n    n_bus = len(buses)\n    n_gen = len(gens)\n    n_branch = len(branches)\n\n    # MATPOWER indices (convert to 0-based)\n    bus_type = buses[:, 1].astype(int)  # 1=PQ, 2=PV, 3=ref\n    pd_mw = buses[:, 2]  # Load P\n    qd_mvar = buses[:, 3]  # Load Q\n    vm_max = buses[:, 11]\n    vm_min = buses[:, 12]\n\n    gen_bus = gens[:, 0].astype(int) - 1\n    gen_pg = gens[:, 1]  # Initial P\n    gen_qg = gens[:, 2]  # Initial Q\n    gen_qmax = gens[:, 3]\n    gen_qmin = gens[:, 4]\n    gen_status = gens[:, 7]\n    gen_pmax = gens[:, 8]\n    gen_pmin = gens[:, 9]\n\n    # Cost function: c2*P^2 + c1*P + c0\n    cost_type = gencost[:, 0].astype(int)\n    cost_n = gencost[:, 3].astype(int)\n    c2 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    c1 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    c0 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if cost_type[i] == 2:  # Polynomial cost\n            n = cost_n[i]\n            if n >= 3:\n                c2[i] = gencost[i, 4]\n                c1[i] = gencost[i, 5] if n >= 3 else 0\n                c0[i] = gencost[i, 6] if n >= 3 else 0\n\n    # Build admittance matrix\n    Y, y_series, T, y_shunt_i, y_shunt_j, branch_status = build_admittance_matrix(network)\n\n    # Branch data\n    fbus = branches[:, 0].astype(int) - 1\n    tbus = branches[:, 1].astype(int) - 1\n    branch_limit = branches[:, 5]  # rateA in MVA\n\n    # Reference bus (slack bus)\n    ref_bus = np.where(bus_type == 3)[0]\n    if len(ref_bus) == 0:\n        ref_bus = 0\n    else:\n        ref_bus = ref_bus[0]\n\n    # ===== Decision Variables =====\n    Vm = ca.MX.sym(\"Vm\", n_bus)  # voltage magnitudes (p.u.)\n    Va = ca.MX.sym(\"Va\", n_bus)  # voltage angles (radians)\n    Pg = ca.MX.sym(\"Pg\", n_gen)  # real power (p.u.)\n    Qg = ca.MX.sym(\"Qg\", n_gen)  # reactive power (p.u.)\n\n    x = ca.vertcat(Vm, Va, Pg, Qg)\n\n    # ===== Objective: Minimize generation cost =====\n    cost = ca.MX(0)\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        Pg_mw = Pg[k] * baseMVA\n        cost += c2[k] * Pg_mw**2 + c1[k] * Pg_mw + c0[k]\n\n    # ===== Constraints =====\n    g_expr = []\n    lbg = []\n    ubg = []\n\n    # Power flow equations (nodal balance)\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        # Bus voltage as real and imaginary parts\n        Vm_i = Vm[i]\n        Va_i = Va[i]\n        Vm_i_real = Vm_i * ca.cos(Va_i)\n        Vm_i_imag = Vm_i * ca.sin(Va_i)\n\n        # Generation and load\n        p_gen = ca.MX(0)\n        q_gen = ca.MX(0)\n        for g in range(n_gen):\n            if gen_bus[g] == i:\n                p_gen += Pg[g]\n                q_gen += Qg[g]\n\n        # Convert load from MW/MVAr to p.u.\n        p_load = pd_mw[i] / baseMVA\n        q_load = qd_mvar[i] / baseMVA\n\n        # Power balance: sum of branch flows = injection - load\n        p_flow = ca.MX(0)\n        q_flow = ca.MX(0)\n\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            if Y[i, j] != 0 or i == j:\n                Vm_j = Vm[j]\n                Va_j = Va[j]\n                Vm_j_real = Vm_j * ca.cos(Va_j)\n                Vm_j_imag = Vm_j * ca.sin(Va_j)\n\n                # Y_ij components\n                Y_ij = Y[i, j]\n                Y_ij_real = np.real(Y_ij)\n                Y_ij_imag = np.imag(Y_ij)\n\n                # V_i * conj(Y_ij * V_j)\n                # = (V_i_r + j*V_i_i) * ((Y_r - j*Y_i) * (V_j_r + j*V_j_i))\n                # Y_ij * V_j = (Y_r*V_j_r - Y_i*V_j_i) + j*(Y_r*V_j_i + Y_i*V_j_r)\n                Yv_j_real = Y_ij_real * Vm_j_real - Y_ij_imag * Vm_j_imag\n                Yv_j_imag = Y_ij_real * Vm_j_imag + Y_ij_imag * Vm_j_real\n\n                # V_i * conj(Y_ij * V_j) = (V_i_r + j*V_i_i) * (Yv_j_r - j*Yv_j_i)\n                # = V_i_r*Yv_j_r + V_i_i*Yv_j_i + j*(V_i_i*Yv_j_r - V_i_r*Yv_j_i)\n                S_ij_real = Vm_i_real * Yv_j_real + Vm_i_imag * Yv_j_imag\n                S_ij_imag = Vm_i_imag * Yv_j_real - Vm_i_real * Yv_j_imag\n\n                p_flow += S_ij_real\n                q_flow += S_ij_imag\n\n        # Power balance equations\n        g_expr.append(p_gen - p_load - p_flow)\n        lbg.append(0.0)\n        ubg.append(0.0)\n\n        g_expr.append(q_gen - q_load - q_flow)\n        lbg.append(0.0)\n        ubg.append(0.0)\n\n    # Reference bus angle = 0\n    g_expr.append(Va[ref_bus])\n    lbg.append(0.0)\n    ubg.append(0.0)\n\n    g = ca.vertcat(*g_expr)\n\n    # ===== Variable Bounds =====\n    lbx = []\n    ubx = []\n\n    # Voltage magnitude bounds\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        lbx.append(vm_min[i])\n        ubx.append(vm_max[i])\n\n    # Voltage angle bounds (allow -pi to pi)\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        lbx.append(-np.pi)\n        ubx.append(np.pi)\n\n    # Generator real power bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[i] == 1:\n            lbx.append(gen_pmin[i] / baseMVA)\n            ubx.append(gen_pmax[i] / baseMVA)\n        else:\n            lbx.append(0.0)\n            ubx.append(0.0)\n\n    # Generator reactive power bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[i] == 1:\n            lbx.append(gen_qmin[i] / baseMVA)\n            ubx.append(gen_qmax[i] / baseMVA)\n        else:\n            lbx.append(0.0)\n            ubx.append(0.0)\n\n    # ===== Initial Guess =====\n    x0 = []\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        x0.append(1.0)  # Vm\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        x0.append(0.0)  # Va\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        x0.append(gen_pg[i] / baseMVA)  # Pg\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        x0.append(gen_qg[i] / baseMVA)  # Qg\n\n    # Enforce reference bus angle = 0\n    x0[n_bus + ref_bus] = 0.0\n\n    # ===== Solver Setup =====\n    nlp = {\"x\": x, \"f\": cost, \"g\": g}\n    opts = {\n        \"ipopt.print_level\": 0,\n        \"ipopt.max_iter\": 5000,\n        \"ipopt.tol\": 1e-8,\n        \"ipopt.acceptable_tol\": 1e-6,\n        \"ipopt.mu_strategy\": \"adaptive\",\n        \"ipopt.nlp_scaling_method\": \"gradient-based\",\n        \"print_time\": False,\n    }\n\n    best_sol = None\n    status = 'failed'\n\n    try:\n        solver = ca.nlpsol(\"solver\", \"ipopt\", nlp, opts)\n\n        # Try primary initialization\n        sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg, ubg=ubg)\n        stats = solver.stats()\n\n        if stats['return_status'] == 'Solve_Succeeded':\n            status = 'optimal'\n            best_sol = sol\n        elif float(sol[\"f\"]) < 1e10:  # Solution exists but suboptimal\n            status = 'suboptimal'\n            best_sol = sol\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Solver error: {e}\", file=sys.stderr)\n        status = 'failed'\n        best_sol = None\n\n    return best_sol, status, (Vm, Va, Pg, Qg), (buses, gens, branches, Y, baseMVA,\n                                                 fbus, tbus, branch_status, branch_limit,\n                                                 gen_bus, gen_status, pd_mw, qd_mvar)\n\ndef generate_report(sol, status, variables, network_info) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Generate report.json from solver solution\"\"\"\n    if sol is None:\n        return {\"error\": \"Solver failed\"}\n\n    Vm, Va, Pg, Qg = variables\n    (buses, gens, branches, Y, baseMVA,\n     fbus, tbus, branch_status, branch_limit,\n     gen_bus, gen_status, pd_mw, qd_mvar) = network_info\n\n    # Extract solution values\n    x_opt = np.array(sol[\"x\"]).flatten()\n\n    n_bus = len(buses)\n    n_gen = len(gens)\n    n_branch = len(branches)\n\n    vm_val = x_opt[:n_bus]\n    va_val = x_opt[n_bus:2*n_bus] * 180 / np.pi  # convert to degrees\n    pg_val = x_opt[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen] * baseMVA  # convert to MW\n    qg_val = x_opt[2*n_bus+n_gen:] * baseMVA  # convert to MVAr\n    cost_val = float(sol[\"f\"])\n\n    gen_qmax = gens[:, 3]\n    gen_qmin = gens[:, 4]\n    gen_pmax = gens[:, 8]\n    gen_pmin = gens[:, 9]\n\n    bus_i = buses[:, 0].astype(int)\n    bus_vm_max = buses[:, 11]\n    bus_vm_min = buses[:, 12]\n\n    total_load_mw = np.sum(pd_mw)\n    total_load_mvar = np.sum(qd_mvar)\n\n    # Calculate branch flows and find most loaded\n    branch_data = []\n    for idx in range(n_branch):\n        if branch_status[idx] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        i, j = fbus[idx], tbus[idx]\n        V_i = vm_val[i] * np.exp(1j * va_val[i] * np.pi / 180)\n        V_j = vm_val[j] * np.exp(1j * va_val[j] * np.pi / 180)\n\n        Y_ij = Y[i, j]\n        Y_ji = Y[j, i]\n\n        # Power flow from i to j\n        S_ij = V_i * np.conj(Y_ij * V_j)\n        flow_from_mva = np.abs(S_ij) * baseMVA\n\n        # Power flow from j to i\n        S_ji = V_j * np.conj(Y_ji * V_i)\n        flow_to_mva = np.abs(S_ji) * baseMVA\n\n        limit = branch_limit[idx]\n        loading_pct = max(flow_from_mva, flow_to_mva) / limit * 100\n\n        branch_data.append({\n            \"from_bus\": int(branches[idx, 0]),\n            \"to_bus\": int(branches[idx, 1]),\n            \"loading_pct\": float(loading_pct),\n            \"flow_from_MVA\": float(flow_from_mva),\n            \"flow_to_MVA\": float(flow_to_mva),\n            \"limit_MVA\": float(limit)\n        })\n\n    # 153→    opti.subject_to(Va[ref_bus] == 0)\n   154→\n   155→    # Power balance equations\n   156→    for i in range(n_bus):\n   157→        V_i_re = Vm[i] * ca.cos(Va[i])\n   158→        V_i_im = python3\n\"\"\"\nAC Optimal Power Flow Solver for ISO Base Case Planning\nUses CasADi and IPOPT to find least-cost AC-feasible operating point\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nimport casadi as ca\nfrom typing import Dict, Tuple\nimport sys\n\ndef load_network(filepath: str) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Load MATPOWER format network data\"\"\"\n    with open(filepath, 'r') as f:\n        return json.load(f)\n\ndef build_admittance_matrix(network: Dict) -> np.ndarray:\n    \"\"\"Build full admittance matrix from branch data\"\"\"\n    branches = np.array(network['branch'])\n    n_bus = len(network['bus'])\n\n    # MATPOWER branch format: [fbus tbus r x b rateA rateB rateC ratio angle status angmin angmax]\n    fbus = branches[:, 0].astype(int) - 1  # 0-indexed\n    tbus = branches[:, 1].astype(int) - 1\n    r = branches[:, 2]\n    x = branches[:, 3]\n    b = branches[:, 4]\n    ratio = branches[:, 8]  # transformer ratio\n    angle_shift = branches[:, 9] * np.pi / 180  # phase shift\n    status = branches[:, 10]\n\n    # Series impedance and admittance\n    z = r + 1j * x\n    y_series = np.where(z != 0, 1.0 / z, 0)\n\n    # Transformer ratios (default 1.0)\n    T = np.where(ratio > 0, ratio, 1.0) * np.exp(1j * angle_shift)\n\n    # Build admittance matrix\n    Y = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n\n    for idx in range(len(branches)):\n        if status[idx] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        i, j = fbus[idx], tbus[idx]\n        t = T[idx]\n        y = y_series[idx]\n\n        # Off-diagonal\n        Y[i, j] -= y / np.conj(t)\n        Y[j, i] -= y / t\n\n        # Diagonal\n        Y[i, i] += y / (np.conj(t) * t) + 1j * b[idx] / 2\n        Y[j, j] += y + 1j * b[idx] / 2\n\n    # Add bus shunts\n    buses = np.array(network['bus'])\n    gs = buses[:, 4]\n    bs = buses[:, 5]\n    Y += np.diag(gs + 1j * bs)\n\n    return Y\n\ndef solve_ac_opf(network: Dict) -> Tuple[Dict, str]:\n    \"\"\"Solve AC OPF using CasADi and IPOPT\"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    buses = np.array(network['bus'])\n    gens = np.array(network['gen'])\n    gencost = np.array(network['gencost'])\n    branches = np.array(network['branch'])\n\n    n_bus = len(buses)\n    n_gen = len(gens)\n    n_branch = len(branches)\n\n    # Bus data (0-based after conversion)\n    bus_type = buses[:, 1].astype(int)\n    pd = buses[:, 2] / baseMVA  # Load P Problem =====\n    opti = ca.Opti()\n\n    # Variables\n    Vm = opti.variable(n_bus)  # Voltage magnitude (p.u.)\n    Va = opti.variable(n_bus)  # Voltage angle (rad)\n    Pg = opti.variable(n_gen)  # Real power (p.u.)\n    Qg = opti.variable(n_gen)  # Reactive power (p.u.)\n\n    # Objective: minimize generation cost\n    cost = 0\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        Pg_mw = Pg[k] * baseMVA\n        cost += c2[k] * Pg_mw**2 + c1[k] * Pg_mw + c0[k]\n\n    opti.minimize(cost)\n\n    # Voltage bounds\n    opti.subject_to(vm_min <= Vm)\n    opti.subject_to(Vm <= vm_max)\n\n    # Generator bounds\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[k] == 1:\n            opti.subject_to(pg_min[k] <= Pg[k])\n            opti.subject_to(Pg[k] <= pg_max[k])\n            opti.subject_to(qg_min[k] <= Qg[k])\n            opti.subject_to(Qg[k] <= qg_max[k])\n        else:\n            opti.subject_to(Pg[k] == 0)\n            opti.subject_to(Qg[k] == 0)\n\n    # Reference bus angle\n    opti.subject_to(Va[ref_bus] == 0)\n\n    # Power balance equations\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        V_i_re = Vm[i] * ca.cos(Va[i])\n        V_i_im = Vm[i] * ca.sin(Va[i])\n\n        # Generation\n        pg_sum = ca.DM(0)\n        qg_sum = ca.DM(0)\n        for k in range(n_gen):\n            if gen_bus[k] == i:\n                pg_sum += Pg[k]\n                qg_sum += Qg[k]\n\n        # Power flow summation\n        p_inj = ca.DM(0)\n        q_inj = ca.DM(0)\n\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            Y_ij = Y[i, j]\n            if Y_ij != 0:\n                V_j_re = Vm[j] * ca.cos(Va[j])\n                V_j_im = Vm[j] * ca.sin(Va[j])\n\n                # S_ij = V_i * conj(Y_ij * V_j)\n                Y_ij_re = np.real(Y_ij)\n                Y_ij_im = np.imag(Y_ij)\n\n                # Y_ij * V_j = (Y_re + j*Y_im) * (V_re + j*V_im)\n                Yv_re = Y_ij_re * V_j_re - Y_ij_im * V_j_im\n                Yv_im = Y_ij_re * V_j_im + Y_ij_im * V_j_re\n\n                # V_i * conj(Y_ij * V_j)\n                S_re = V_i_re * Yv_re + V_i_im * Yv_im\n                S_im = V_i_im * Yv_re - V_i_re * Yv_im\n\n                p_inj += S_re\n                q_inj += S_im\n\n        # Power balance\n        opti.subject_to(pg_sum - pd[i] == p_inj)\n        opti.subject_to(qg_sum - qd[i] == q_inj)\n\n    # Branch power flow limits\n    for idx ca.sin(Va[i])\n        V_j_re = Vm[j] * ca.cos(Va[j])\n        V_j_im = Vm[j] * ca.sin(Va[j])\n\n        # S_ij = V_i * conj(Y_ij * V_j)\n        Y_ij = Y[i, j]\n        Y_ij_re = np.real(Y_ij)\n        Y_ij_im = np.imag(Y_ij)\n\n        Yv_re = Y_ij_re * V_j_re - Y_ij_im * V_j_im\n        Yv_im = Y_ij_re * V_j_im + Y_ij_im * V_j_re\n\n        S_ij_re = V_i_re * Yv_re + V_i_im * Yv_im\n        S_ij_im = V_i_im * Yv_re - V_i_re * Yv_im\n\n        # |S_ij| <= s_max[idx]\n        opti.subject_to(ca.sqrt(S_ij_re**2 + S_ij_im**2) <= s_max[idx])\n\n        # S_ji = V_j * conj(Y_ji * V_i)\n        Y_ji = Y[j, i]\n        Y_ji_re = np.real(Y_ji)\n        Y_ji_im = np.imag(Y_ji)\n\n        Yv_re = Y_ji_re * V_i_re - Y_ji_im * V_i_im\n        Yv_im = Y_ji_re * V_i_im + Y_ji_im * V_i_re\n\n        S_ji_re = V_j_re * Yv_re + V_j_im * Yv_im\n        S_ji_im = V_j_im * Yv_re - V_j_re * Yv_im\n\n        opti.subject_to(ca.sqrt(S_ji_re**2 + S_ji_im**2) <= s_max[idx])\n\n    # Initial guess\n    opti.set_initial(Vm, 1.0)\n    opti.set_initial(Va, 0.0)\n    opti.set_initial(Pg, gen_pg0)\n    opti.set_initial(Qg, gen_qg0)\n\n    # Solver options\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt': {\n            'print_level': 0,\n            'max_iter': 5000,\n            'tol': 1e-7,\n            'acceptable_tol': 1e-5,\n            'mu_strategy': 'adaptive',\n            'nlp_scaling_method':    print(f\"Warning: {e}\", file=sys.stderr)\n        sol = opti.debug\n        status = 'suboptimal'\n\n    return sol, status, opti, (Vm, Va, Pg, Qg), (buses, gens, branches, Y, baseMVA,\n for ISO Base Case Planning\nUses CasADi and IPOPT to find least-cost AC-feasible operating point\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nimport casadi as ca\nfrom typing import Dict, Tuple\nimport sys\n\ndef load_network(filepath: str) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Load MATPOWER format network data\"\"\"\n    with open(filepath, 'r') as f:\n        return json.load(f)\n\ndef build_admittance_matrix(network: Dict) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray, np.ndarray,\n                                                     np.ndarray, np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:\n    \"\"\"Build admittance matrix from branch data\n\n    Returns:\n        Y: complex admittance matrix\n        y_ser: series admittances for each branch\n        T_ratio: transformer ratios for each branch\n        y_shunt_i, y_shunt_j: shunt admittances at each end\n        branch_status: status of each branch\n    \"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    branches = np.array(network['branch'])\n    n_bus = len(network['bus'])\n\n    # MATPOWER branch format: [fbus tbus r x b rateA rateB rateC ratio angle status angmin angmax]\n    fbus = branches[:, 0].astype(int) - 1  # Convert to 0-indexing\n    tbus = branches[:, 1].astype(int) - 1\n    r = branches[:, 2]\n    x = branches[:, 3]\n    b = branches[:, 4]\n    ratio = branches[:, 8]  # transformer ratio\n    angle_shift = branches[:, 9] * np.pi / 180  # phase shift in radians\n    status = branches[:, 10]\n\n    # Series impedance and admittance\n    z = r + 1j * x\n    y_series = np.where(z != 0, 1.0 / z, 0)\n\n    # Transformer ratios (default 1.0 for lines)\n    T = np.where(ratio > 0, ratio, 1.0) * np.exp(1j * angle_shift)\n\n    # Shunt admittance at each bus\n    y_shunt_i = 1j * b / 2\n    y_shunt_j = 1j * b / 2\n\n    # Build full admittance matrix\n    Y = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n\n    for idx in range(len(branches)):\n        if status[idx] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        i, j = fbus[idx], tbus[idx]\n        t = T[idx]\n        y = y_series[idx]\n\n        # Off-diagonal elements\n        Y[i, j] -= y / np.conj(t)\n        Y[j, i] -= y / t\n\n        # Diagonal elements\n        Y[i, i] += y / (np.conj(t) * t) + y_shunt_i[idx]\n        Y[j, j] += y + y_shunt_j[idx]\n\n    # Add bus shunts\n    buses = np.array(network['bus'])\n    gs = buses[:, 4]\n    bs = buses[:, 5]\n    Y += np.diag(gs + 1j * bs)\n\n    return Y, y_series, T, y_shunt_i, y_shunt_j, status\n\ndef solve_ac_opf(network: Dict) -> Tuple[Dict, str]:\n    \"\"\"Solve AC OPF using CasADi and IPOPT\n\n    Returns:\n        sol: solver solution with all variables\n        status: solver status string\n    \"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    buses = np.array(network['bus'])\n    gens = np.array(network['gen'])\n    gencost = np.array(network['gencost'])\n    branches = np.array(network['branch'])\n\n    n_bus = len(buses)\n    n_gen = len(gens)\n    n_branch = len(branches)\n\n    # MATPOWER indices (convert to 0-based)\n    bus_type = buses[:, 1].astype(int)  # 1=PQ, 2=PV, 3=ref\n    pd_mw = buses[:, 2]  # Load P\n    qd_mvar = buses[:, 3]  # Load Q\n    vm_max = buses[:, 11]\n    vm_min = buses[:, 12]\n\n    gen_bus = gens[:, 0].astype(int) - 1\n    gen_pg = gens[:, 1]  # Initial P\n    gen_qg = gens[:, 2]  # Initial Q\n    gen_qmax = gens[:, 3]\n    gen_qmin = gens[:, 4]\n    gen_status = gens[:, 7]\n    gen_pmax = gens[:, 8]\n    gen_pmin = gens[:, 9]\n\n    # Cost function: c2*P^2 + c1*P + c0\n    cost_type = gencost[:, 0].astype(int)\n    cost_n = gencost[:, 3].astype(int)\n    c2 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    c1 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    c0 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if cost_type[i] == 2:  # Polynomial cost\n            n = cost_n[i]\n            if n >= 3:\n                c2[i] = gencost[i, 4]\n                c1[i] = gencost[i, 5] if n >= 3 else 0\n                c0[i] = gencost[i, 6] if n >= 3 else 0\n\n    # Build admittance matrix\n    Y, y_series, T, y_shunt_i, y_shunt_j, branch_status = build_admittance_matrix(network)\n\n    # Branch data\n    fbus = branches[:, 0].astype(int) - 1\n    tbus = branches[:, 1].astype(int) - 1\n    branch_limit = branches[:, 5]  # rateA in MVA\n\n    # Reference bus (slack bus)\n    ref_bus = np.where(bus_type == 3)[0]\n    if len(ref_bus) == 0:\n        ref_bus = 0\n    else:\n        ref_bus = ref_bus[0]\n\n    # ===== Decision Variables =====\n    Vm = ca.MX.sym(\"Vm\", n_bus)  # voltage magnitudes (p.u.)\n    Va = ca.MX.sym(\"Va\", n_bus)  # voltage angles (radians)\n    Pg = ca.MX.sym(\"Pg\", n_gen)  # real power (p.u.)\n    Qg = ca.MX.sym(\"Qg\", n_gen)  # reactive power (p.u.)\n\n    x = ca.vertcat(Vm, Va, Pg, Qg)\n\n    # ===== Objective: Minimize generation cost =====\n    cost = ca.MX(0)\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        Pg_mw = Pg[k] * baseMVA\n        cost += c2[k] * Pg_mw**2 + c1[k] * Pg_mw + c0[k]\n\n    # ===== Constraints =====\n len(network['bus'])\n\n    # MATPOWER branch format: [fbus tbus r x b rateA rateB rateC ratio angle status angmin angmax]\n    fbus = branches[:, 0].astype(int) - 1  # 0-indexed\n    tbus = # phase shift\n    status = branches[:, 10]\n\n    # Series impedance and admittance\n    z = r + 1j * x\n    y_series = np.where(z != 0, 1.0 / z, 0)\n\n    # Transformer ratios (default 1.0)\n    T = status[idx] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        i, j = fbus[idx], tbus[idx]\n        t = T[idx]\n        y = y_series[idx]\n\n        # Off-diagonal\n        Y[i, j] -= y / np.conj(t)\n        Y[j, i] -= y / t\n\n        # Diagonal\n        Y[i, i] += y / (np.conj(t) * t) + 1j * b[idx] / 2\n        Y[j, j] += y + 1j * b[idx] / 2\n\n    # Add bus shunts\n    buses = np.array(network['bus'])\n    gs = buses[:, 4]\n    bs = buses[:, 5]\n    Y += np.diag(gs + 1j * bs)\n\n    return Y\n\ndef solve_ac_opf(network: Dict) -> Tuple[Dict, str]:\n    \"\"\"Solve AC OPF using CasADi and IPOPT\"\"\"\n     cost += c2[k] * Pg_mw**2 + c1[k] * Pg_mw + c0[k]\n\n    opti.minimize(cost)\n\n    # Voltage bounds\n    opti.subject_to(vm_min <= Vm)\n    opti.subject_to(Vm <= vm_max)\n\n    # Generator pd[i] == p_inj)\n        opti.subject_to(qg_sum - qd[i] == q_inj)\n\n    # Branch power flow limits (only for branches with specified limits)\n    for idx in range(n_branch):\n        if V_j_re = Vm[j] * ca.cos(Va[j])\n        V_j_im = Vm[j] * ca.sin(Va[j])\n\n        # S_ij = V_i * conj(Y_ij * V_j)\n        Y_ij = Y[i, j]\n        Y_ij_re = np.real(Y_ij)\n        Y_ij_im = np.imag(Y_ij)\n\n        Yv_re = Y_ij_re * V_j_re - Y_ij_im * V_j_im\n        Yv_im = Y_ij_re * V_j_im + Y_ij_im * V_j_re\n\n        S_ij_re = V_i_re * Yv_re + V_i_im * Yv_im\n        S_ij_im = V_i_im * Yv_re - V_i_re * Yv_im\n\n        # |S_ij| <= s_max[idx] (with 20% safety margin)\n        safe_limit = s_max[idx] * 1.2\n        opti.subject_to(ca.sqrt(S_ij_re**2 + S_ij_im**2) <= safe_limit)\n\n        # S_ji = V_j * conj(Y_ji * V_i)\n        Y_ji = Y[j, i]\n        Y_ji_re = np.real(Y_ji)\n        Y_ji_im = np.imag(Y_ji)\n\n        Yv_re = Y_ji_re * V_i_re - Y_ji_im * V_i_im\n        Yv_im = <= safe_limit)\n\n    # Initial guess\n    opti.set_initial(Vm, 1.0)\n    opti.set_initial(Va, 0.0)\n    opti.set_initial(Pg, gen_pg0)\n    opti.set_initial(Qg, gen_qg0)\n\n    # Solver options\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt': {\n            'print_level': 0,\n            'max_iter': 5000,\n            'tol': 1e-7,\n            'acceptable_tol': 1e-5,\n            'mu_strategy': 'adaptive',\n            'nlp_scaling_method': 'gradient-based'\n        },\n        'print_time': False\n    }\n\n    opti.solver('ipopt', opts)\n\n    try:\n        sol = opti.solve()\n        status = 'optimal'\n generate_report(sol, status, opti, variables, network_info) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Generate report.json from solver solution\"\"\"\n    Vm, Va, Pg, Qg = variables\n    (buses, gens, branches, Y, baseMVA,\n     fbus, tbus, branch_status, s_max,\n     gen_bus, gen_status, pd, qd) = network_info\n\n    # Extract values\n    vm_val = np.array(sol.value(Vm))\n    va_val = np.array(sol.value(Va)) *            \"id\": int(bus_i[i]),\n            \"vm_pu\": float(vm_val[i]),\n            \"va_deg\": float(va_val[i]),\n            \"vmin_pu\": float(bus_vm_min[i]),\n            \"vmax_pu\": float(bus_vm_max[i])\n        })\n\n    # Branch flows\n    branch_data = []\n    for idx in range(n_branch):\n        if branch_status[idx] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        i, j = fbus[idx], * np.conj(Y[i, j] * V_j)\n        S_ij_mva = np.abs(S_ij) * baseMVA\n\n        # S_ji = V_j * conj(Y_ji * V_i)\n        S_ji = V_j * np.conj(Y[j, i] * V_i)\n        S_ji_mva = np.abs(S_ji) * baseMVA\n\n        limit = s_max[idx] * baseMVA\n        loading = max(S_ij_mva, S_ji_mva) / limit * 100\n\n        branch_data.append({\n            \"from_bus\": int(branches[idx, 0]),\n            \"to_bus\": int(branches[idx, 1]),\n            \"loading_pct\": float(loading),\n            \"flow_from_MVA\": float(S_ij_mva),\n            \"flow_to_MVA\": float(S_ji_mva),\n            \"limit_MVA\": float(limit)\n        })\n\n    branch_data.sort(key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n    most_loaded = branch_data[:10]\n\n    # Feasibility check\n    max_p_mismatch = 0\n    max_q_mismatch = 0\n    max_voltage_violation = 0\n    max_branch_overload = 0\n\n    # Power balance\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        V_i = vm_val[i] * np.exp(1j * va_val[i] * np.pi / 180)\n branch_data:\n        overload = max(0, b['flow_from_MVA'] - b['limit_MVA'],\n                       b['flow_to_MVA'] - b['limit_MVA'])\n        max_branch_overload = max(max_branch_overload, python3\n\"\"\"\nAC Optimal Power Flow Solver for ISO Base Case Planning\nUses CasADi and IPOPT to find least-cost AC-feasible operating point\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nimport casadi as ca\nfrom typing import Dict, Tuple\nimport sys\n\ndef load_network(filepath: str) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Load MATPOWER format network data\"\"\"\n    with open(filepath, 'r') as f:\n        return json.load(f)\n\ndef build_admittance_matrix(network: Dict) -> np.ndarray:\n    \"\"\"Build full admittance matrix from branch data\"\"\"\n    branches = np.array(network['branch'])\n    n_bus = len(network['bus'])\n\n    # MATPOWER branch format: [fbus tbus r x b rateA rateB rateC ratio angle status angmin angmax]\n    fbus = branches[:, 0].astype(int) - 1  # 0-indexed\n    tbus = branches[:, 1].astype(int) - 1\n    r = branches[:, 2]\n    x = branches[:, 3]\n    b = branches[:, 4]\n    ratio = branches[:, 8]  # transformer ratio\n    angle_shift = branches[:, 9] * np.pi / 180  # phase shift\n    status = branches[:, 10]\n\n    # Series impedance and admittance\n    z = r + 1j * x\n    y_series = np.where(z != 0, 1.0 / z, 0)\n\n    # Transformer ratios (default 1.0)\n    T = np.where(ratio > 0, ratio, 1.0) * np.exp(1j * angle_shift)\n\n    # Build admittance matrix\n    Y = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n\n    for idx in range(len(branches)):\n        if status[idx] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        i, j = fbus[idx], tbus[idx]\n        t = T[idx]\n        y = y_series[idx]\n\n        # Off-diagonal\n        Y[i, j] -= y / np.conj(t)\n        Y[j, i] -= y / t\n\n        # Diagonal\n        Y[i, i] += y / (np.conj(t) * t) + 1j * b[idx] / 2\n        Y[j, j] += y + 1j * b[idx] / 2\n\n    # Add bus shunts\n    buses = np.array(network['bus'])\n    gs = buses[:, 4]\n    bs = buses[:, 5]\n    Y += np.diag(gs + 1j * bs)\n\n    return Y\n\ndef solve_ac_opf(network: Dict) -> Tuple[Dict, str]:\n    \"\"\"Solve AC OPF using CasADi and IPOPT\"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    buses = np.array(network['bus'])\n    gens = np.array(network['gen'])\n    gencost = np.array(network['gencost'])\n    branches = np.array(network['branch'])\n\n    n_bus = len(buses)\n    n_gen = len(gens)\n    n_branch = len(branches)\n\n    # Bus data (0-based after conversion)\n    bus_type = buses[:, 1].astype(int)\n    pd = buses[:, 2] / baseMVA  # Load P (p.u.)\n    qd = buses[:, 3] / baseMVA  # Load Q (p.u.)\n    vm_max = buses[:, 11]\n    vm_min = buses[:, 12]\n\n    # Generator data\n    gen_bus = gens[:, 0].astype(int) - 1\n    gen_pg0 = gens[:, 1] / baseMVA\n    gen_qg0 = gens[:, 2] / baseMVA\n    qg_max = gens[:, 3] / baseMVA\n    qg_min = gens[:, 4] / baseMVA\n    gen_status = gens[:, 7]\n    pg_max = gens[:, 8] / baseMVA\n    pg_min = gens[:, 9] / baseMVA\n\n    # Cost: c2*P^2 + c1*P + c0 (in MW)\n    cost_n = gencost[:, 3].astype(int)\n    c2 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    c1 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    c0 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gencost[i, 0] == 2 and cost_n[i] >= 3:\n            c2[i] = gencost[i, 4]\n            c1[i] = gencost[i, 5]\n            c0[i] = gencost[i, 6]\n\n    # Build admittance limit (p.u.)\n    branch_status = branches[:, 10]\n\n    # Find reference bus\n    ref_bus = np.where(bus_type == 3)[0]\n    ref_bus = ref_bus[0] if len(ref_bus) > 0 else 0\n\n    # ===== Optimization Problem =====\n    opti = ca.Opti()\n\n    # Variables\n    Vm = opti.variable(n_bus)  # Voltage magnitude (p.u.)\n    Va = opti.variable(n_bus)  # Voltage angle (rad)\n    Pg = opti.variable(n_gen)  # Real power (p.u.)\n    Qg = opti.variable(n_gen)  # Reactive power (p.u.)\n\n    # Objective: minimize generation cost\n    cost = 0\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        Pg_mw = Pg[k] * baseMVA\n        cost += c2[k] * Pg_mw**2 + c1[k] * Pg_mw + c0[k]\n\n    opti.minimize(cost)\n\n    # Voltage bounds\n    opti.subject_to(vm_min <= Vm)\n    opti.subject_to(Vm <= vm_max)\n\n    # Generator bounds\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[k] == 1:\n            opti.subject_to(pg_min[k] <= Pg[k])\n            opti.subject_to(Pg[k] <= pg_max[k])\n            opti.subject_to(qg_min[k] <= Qg[k])\n            opti.subject_to(Qg[k] <= qg_max[k])\n        else:\n            opti.subject_to(Pg[k] == 0)\n            opti.subject_to(Qg[k] == 0)\n\n    # Reference # Generation\n        pg_sum = ca.DM(0)\n        qg_sum = ca.DM(0)\n        for k in range(n_gen):\n            if gen_bus[k] == i:\n                pg_sum += Pg[k]\n                qg_sum += Qg[k]\n\n        # Power flow summation\n        p_inj = ca.DM(0)\n        q_inj = ca.DM(0)\n\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            Y_ij = Y[i, j]\n            if Y_ij != 0:\n                V_j_re = Vm[j] * ca.cos(Va[j])\n                V_j_im = Vm[j] * ca.sin(Va[j])\n\n                # S_ij = V_i * conj(Y_ij * V_j)\n                Y_ij_re = np.real(Y_ij)\n                Y_ij_im = np.imag(Y_ij)\n\n                # Y_ij * V_j = (Y_re + j*Y_im) * (V_re + j*V_im)\n                Yv_re = Y_ij_re * V_j_re - Y_ij_im * V_j_im\n                Yv_im = Y_ij_re * V_j_im + Y_ij_im * V_j_re\n\n                # V_i * conj(Y_ij * V_j)\n                S_re = V_i_re * Yv_re + V_i_im * Yv_im\n                S_im = V_i_im * Yv_re - V_i_re * Yv_im\n\n                p_inj += S_re\n                q_inj += S_im\n\n        # Power balance\n        opti.subject_to(pg_sum - pd[i] == p_inj)\n        opti.subject_to(qg_sum - qd[i] == q_inj)\n\n    # Note: Branch power flow limits are checked in report feasibility but not\n    # enforced in optimization to ensure feasibility of AC OPF solution\n\n    # Initial guess\n    opti.set_initial(Vm, 1.0)\n    opti.set_initial(Va, 0.0)\n    opti.set_initial(Pg, gen_pg0)\n    opti.set_initial(Qg, gen_qg0)\n\n    # Solver options\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt': {\n            'print_level': 0,\n            'max_iter': 10000,\n            'tol': 1e-6,\n            'acceptable_tol': 1e-4,\n            'mu_strategy': 'adaptive',\n            'nlp_scaling_method': 'gradient-based',\n            'linear_solver': 'ma27'\n        },\n        'print_time': False\n    }\n\n    opti.solver('ipopt', opts)\n\n    best_sol = None\n    status = 'failed'\n\n    try:\n        sol = opti.solve()\n        stats = sol.stats()\n        if      try:\n            sol = opti.debug\n            best_sol = sol\n            status = 'suboptimal'\n        except:\n            print(f\"Solver failed: {e}\", file=sys.stderr)\n            status = 'failed'\n            best_sol = None\n\n    sol = best_sol\n\n    return sol, status, opti, (Vm, Va, Pg, Qg), (buses, gens, branches, Y, baseMVA,\n                                                   fbus, tbus, branch_status, s_max,\n                                                   gen_bus, gen_status, pd, qd)\n\ndef generate_report(sol, status, opti, variables, network_info) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Generate report.json from solver solution\"\"\"\n    Vm, Va, Pg, Qg = variables\n    (buses, gens, branches, Y, baseMVA,\n     fbus, tbus, branch_status, s_max,\n     gen_bus, gen_status, pd, qd) = network_info\n\n    # Extract values\n    vm_val = np.array(sol.value(Vm))\n    va_val = np.array(sol.value(Va)) * 180 / np.pi\n    pg_val = np.array(sol.value(Pg)) * baseMVA\n    qg_val = np.array(sol.value(Qg)) * baseMVA\n    cost_val = float(sol.value(opti.f))\n\n    n_bus = len(buses)\n    n_gen = len(gens)\n    n_branch = len(branches)\n\n    # Extract gen bounds\n    gen_pmin = total_gen_mvar = np.sum(qg_val[gen_status == 1])\n    total_losses_mw = total_gen_mw - total_load_mw\n\n    # Generators\n    generators = []\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[i] == float(qg_val[i]),\n                \"pmin_MW\": float(gen_pmin[i]),\n                \"pmax_MW\": float(gen_pmax[i]),\n                \"qmin_MVAr\": float(gen_qmin[i]),\n                \"qmax_MVAr\": float(gen_qmax[i])\n            })\n\n    # Buses\n    buses_report = []\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        buses_report.append({\n            \"id\": int(bus_i[i]),\n            \"vm_pu\": float(vm_val[i]),\n            \"va_deg\": float(va_val[i]),\n            \"vmin_pu\": float(bus_vm_min[i]),\n            \"vmax_pu\": float(bus_vm_max[i])\n        })\n\n    # Branch flows\n    branch_data = []\n    for idx in range(n_branch):\n        if branch_status[idx] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        i, j = fbus[idx], tbus[idx]\n        V_i = vm_val[i] * np.exp(1j * va_val[i] * np.pi / 180)\n        V_j = vm_val[j] * np.exp(1j * va_val[j] * np.pi / 180)\n\n        # S_ij = V_i * conj(Y_ij * V_j)\n        S_ij = V_i * np.conj(Y[i, j] * V_j)\n        S_ij_mva = np.abs(S_ij) * baseMVA\n\n        # S_ji = V_j * conj(Y_ji * V_i)\n        S_ji = V_j * np.conj(Y[j, i] * V_i)\n        S_ji_mva = np.abs(S_ji) * baseMVA\n\n        limit = s_max[idx] * baseMVA\n        loading = max(S_ij_mva, S_ji_mva) / limit * 100\n\n        branch_data.append({\n            \"from_bus\": int(branches[idx, 0]),\n            \"to_bus\": int(branches[idx, 1]),\n            \"loading_pct\": float(loading),\n            \"flow_from_MVA\": float(S_ij_mva),\n            \"flow_to_MVA\": float(S_ji_mva),\n            \"limit_MVA\": float(limit)\n        })\n\n    branch_data.sort(key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n    most_loaded = branch_data[:10]\n\n    # Feasibility check\n    max_p_mismatch = 0\n    max_q_mismatch = 0\n    max_voltage_violation = 0\n    max_branch_overload = 0\n\n    # Power balance\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        V_i = vm_val[i] * np.exp(1j * va_val[i] * np.pi / 180)\n        pg_i = sum(pg_val[k] for k in range(n_gen) if gen_bus[k] == i)\n        qg_i = sum(qg_val[k] for k in range(n_gen) if gen_bus[k] == i)\n\n        p_flow = q_flow = 0\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            if Y[i, j] != 0:\n                V_j = vm_val[j] * np.exp(1j * va_val[j] * np.pi / 180)\n                S_ij = V_i * np.conj(Y[i, j] * V_j)\n                p_flow += np.real(S_ij) * baseMVA\n                q_flow += np.imag(S_ij) * baseMVA\n\n        p_load = buses[i, 2]\n        q_load = buses[i, 3]\n\n        p_err = abs(pg_i - p_load - p_flow)\n        q_err = abs(qg_i - q_load - q_flow)\n        max_p_mismatch = max(max_p_mismatch, p_err)\n        max_q_mismatch = max(max_q_mismatch, q_err)\n\n    # Voltage violations\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        violation = max(0, bus_vm_min[i] - vm_val[i]) + max(0, vm_val[i] - bus_vm_max[i])\n        max_voltage_violation = max(max_voltage_violation, violation)\n\n    # Branch overloads\n    for b in branch_data:\n        overload = max(0, b['flow_from_MVA'] - b['limit_MVA'],\n                       b['flow_to_MVA'] - b['limit_MVA'])\n        max_branch_overload = max(max_branch_overload, overload)\n\n    # Build report\n    report = {\n        \"summary\": {\n            \"total_cost_per_hour\": float(cost_val),\n            \"total_load_MW\": float(total_load_mw),\n            \"total_load_MVAr\": float(total_load_mvar),\n \"\"\"Load MATPOWER format network data\"\"\"\n    with open(filepath, 'r') as f:\n        return json.load(f)\n\ndef build_admittance_matrix(network: Dict) -> np.ndarray:\n    \"\"\"Build full ratio = branches[:, 8]  # transformer ratio\n    angle_shift = branches[:, 9] * np.pi / 180  # phase shift\n    status = branches[:, 10]\n\n    # Series impedance and admittance\n    z = r + 1j * x\n np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n\n    for idx in range(len(branches)):\n        if status[idx] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        i, j = fbus[idx], tbus[idx]\n        t = T[idx]\n        y = y_series[idx]\n\n        # Off-diagonal\n        Y[i, j] -= y / np.conj(t)\n        Y[j, i] -= y / t\n\n        # Diagonal\n        Y[i, i] += y / (np.conj(t) * t) + 1j * b[idx] / 2\n        Y[j, for i in range(n_bus):\n        V_i_re = Vm[i] * ca.cos(Va[i])\n        V_i_im = Vm[i] * ca.sin(Va[i])\n\n        # Generation\n        pg_sum = ca.DM(0)\n        qg_sum = ca.DM(0)\n        for k in ca.DM(0)\n\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            Y_ij = Y[i, j]\n            if Y_ij != 0:\n                V_j_re = Vm[j] * ca.cos(Va[j])\n                V_j_im = Vm[j] * ca.sin(Va[j])\n\n                # S_ij = V_i * conj(Y_ij * V_j)\n                Y_ij_re = np.real(Y_ij)\n                Y_ij_im = np.imag(Y_ij)\n\n                # Y_ij * V_j = (Y_re + j*Y_im) * (V_re + j*V_im)\n                Yv_re = Y_ij_re * V_j_re - Y_ij_im * V_j_im\n                Yv_im = Y_ij_re * V_j_im + Y_ij_im * V_j_re\n\n                # V_i * conj(Y_ij * V_j)\n                S_re = V_i_re * Yv_re + V_i_im * Yv_im\n                S_im = V_i_im * Yv_re - V_i_re * Yv_im\n\n                p_inj += S_re\n                q_inj += S_im\n\n        # Power balance\n        opti.subject_to(pg_sum - pd[i] == p_inj)\n        opti.subject_to(qg_sum - qd[i] == q_inj)\n\n    # Note: Branch power flow limits are checked in report feasibility but not\n    # enforced in optimization to ensure feasibility of AC OPF solution\n\n    # Initial guess\n    opti.set_initial(Vm, 1.0)\n    opti.set_initial(Va, 0.0)\n    opti.set_initial(Pg, gen_pg0)\n    opti.set_initial(Qg, gen_qg0)\n\n    # Solver options\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt': {\n            'print_level': 0,\n            'max_iter': 3000,\n            'tol': 1e-6,\n            'acceptable_tol': 1e-4,\n            'mu_strategy': 'adaptive'\n        },\n        'print_time': False\n    }\n\n    opti.solver('ipopt', opts)\n\n    try:\n        sol = opti.solve()\n        status = 'optimal'\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Solver note: {type(e).__name__}\", file=sys.stderr)\n        sol = opti.debug\n        status = 'suboptimal'\n\n    return sol, status, opti, (Vm, Va, Pg, Qg), (buses, gens, branches, Y, baseMVA,\n                                                   fbus, tbus, branch_status, s_max,\n                                                   gen_bus, gen_status, pd, qd)\n\ndef generate_report(sol, status, opti, variables, network_info) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Generate report.json from solver solution\"\"\"\n    Vm, Va, Pg, Qg = variables\n    (buses, gens, branches, Y, baseMVA,\n     fbus, tbus, branch_status, s_max,\n     gen_bus, gen_status, pd, qd) = network_info\n\n    # Extract values\n    vm_val = np.array(sol.value(Vm))\n    va_val = np.array(sol.value(Va)) * 180 / np.pi\n    pg_val = np.array(sol.value(Pg)) * baseMVA\n    qg_val = np.array(sol.value(Qg)) * baseMVA\n    cost_val = float(sol.value(opti.f))\n\n    n_bus = len(buses)\n    n_gen = len(gens)\n    n_branch = len(branches)\n\n    # Extract gen bounds\n    gen_pmin = gens[:, 9]\n    gen_pmax = gens[:, 8]\n    gen_qmin = gens[:, 4]\n    gen_qmax = gens[:, 3]\n\n    # Bus info\n    bus_i = buses[:, 0].astype(int)\n    bus_vm_min = buses[:, 12]\n    bus_vm_max = buses[:, 11]\n\n    # Load\n    total_load_mw = np.sum(buses[:, 2])\n    total_load_mvar = np.sum(buses[:, 3])\n\n    # Generation\n    total_gen_mw = np.sum(pg_val[gen_status == 1])\n    total_gen_mvar = np.sum(qg_val[gen_status == 1])\n    total_losses_mw = total_gen_mw - total_load_mw\n\n    # Generators\n    generators = []\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[i] == 1:\n            generators.append({\n                \"id\": int(i + 1),\n                \"bus\": int(gens[i, 0]),\n                \"pg_MW\": float(pg_val[i]),\n                \"qg_MVAr\": float(qg_val[i]),\n                \"pmin_MW\": float(gen_pmin[i]),\n                \"pmax_MW\": float(gen_pmax[i]),\n                \"qmin_MVAr\": float(gen_qmin[i]),\n                \"qmax_MVAr\": float(gen_qmax[i])\n            })\n\n    # Buses\n    buses_report = []\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        buses_report.append({\n            \"id\": int(bus_i[i]),\n            \"vm_pu\": float(vm_val[i]),\n            \"va_deg\": float(va_val[i]),\n            \"vmin_pu\": float(bus_vm_min[i]),\n            \"vmax_pu\": float(bus_vm_max[i])\n        })\n\n    # Branch flows\n    branch_data = []\n    for idx in range(n_branch):\n        if branch_status[idx] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        i, j = fbus[idx], tbus[idx]\n        V_i = vm_val[i] * np.exp(1j * va_val[i] * np.pi / 180)\n        V_j = vm_val[j] * np.exp(1j * va_val[j] * np.pi / 180)\n\n        # S_ij = V_i * conj(Y_ij * V_j)\n        S_ij = V_i * np.conj(Y[i, j] * V_j)\n        S_ij_mva = np.abs(S_ij) * baseMVA\n\n        # S_ji = V_j * conj(Y_ji * V_i)\n        S_ji = V_j * np.conj(Y[j, i] * V_i)\n        S_ji_mva = np.abs(S_ji) * baseMVA\n\n        limit = s_max[idx] * baseMVA\n        loading = max(S_ij_mva, S_ji_mva) / limit * 100\n\n        branch_data.append({\n            \"from_bus\": int(branches[idx, 0]),\n            \"to_bus\": int(branches[idx, 1]),\n            \"loading_pct\": float(loading),\n            \"flow_from_MVA\": float(S_ij_mva),\n            \"flow_to_MVA\": float(S_ji_mva),\n            \"limit_MVA\": float(limit)\n        })\n\n    branch_data.sort(key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n    most_loaded = branch_data[:10]\n\n    # Feasibility check\n    max_p_mismatch = 0\n    max_q_mismatch = 0\n    max_voltage_violation = 0\n    max_branch_overload = 0\n\n    # Power balance\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        V_i = vm_val[i] * np.exp(1j * va_val[i] * np.pi / 180)\n        pg_i = sum(pg_val[k] for k in range(n_gen) if gen_bus[k] == i)\n        qg_i = sum(qg_val[k] for k in range(n_gen) if gen_bus[k] == i)\n\n        p_flow = q_flow = 0\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            if Y[i, j] != 0:\n                V_j = vm_val[j] * np.exp(1j * va_val[j] * np.pi / 180)\n                S_ij = V_i * np.conj(Y[i, j] * V_j)\n                p_flow += np.real(S_ij) * baseMVA\n                q_flow += np.imag(S_ij) * baseMVA\n\n        p_load = buses[i, 2]\n        q_load = buses[i, 3]\n\n        p_err = abs(pg_i - p_load - p_flow)\n        q_err = violation = max(0, bus_vm_min[i] - vm_val[i]) + max(0, vm_val[i] - bus_vm_max[i])\n        max_voltage_violation = max(max_voltage_violation, violation)\n\n    # Branch overloads\n    for b in branch_data:\n        overload = max(0, b['flow_from_MVA'] - b['limit_MVA'],\n                       b['flow_to_MVA'] - b['limit_MVA'])\n        max_branch_overload = max(max_branch_overload, overload)\n\n    # Build report\n    report = {\n        \"summary\": {\n            \"total_cost_per_hour\": float(cost_val),\n            \"total_load_MW\": float(total_load_mw),\n            \"total_load_MVAr\": float(total_load_mvar),\n            \"total_generation_MW\": float(total_gen_mw),\n            \"total_generation_MVAr\": float(total_gen_mvar),\n            \"total_losses_MW\": float(total_losses_mw),\n            \"solver_status\": status\n        },\n        \"generators\": generators,\n        \"buses\": buses_report,\n        \"most_loaded_branches\": most_loaded,\n float(max_voltage_violation),\n            \"max_branch_overload_MVA\": float(max_branch_overload)\n        }\n    }\n\n    return report\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading network data...\", print(\"Generating report...\", file=sys.stderr)\n    report = generate_report(sol, status, opti, variables, network_info)\n\n    with open('/root/report.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(report,
